204	### agent.AgentConfig: type
205	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class AgentConfig: memory: bool = True; frozen_copy: bool = True; eps_decay: bool = True; hidden: int = 256; lr: float = 1e-3; batch: int = 64; memory_size: int = 100_000; gamma: float = 0.9; refresh_every: int = 1_000; eps_start: float = 1.0; eps_end: float = 0.01; eps_fixed: float = 0.1`
206	- **Invariants:** the first 3 fields are the ablation switches (ABL-01–ABL-03); `eps_fixed` applies only when `eps_decay` is off; `0 < gamma < 1`
207	- **Boundary:** runs, train, study, registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/agent.AgentConfig/ · **Cases:** the default (full agent); each switch off alone; all 3 off
208	- **Used by:** runs, train, study, registry
209	
210	### agent.DQNAgent: Hand-written DQN
211	```python
212	class DQNAgent(LearningAgent):
213	    def __init__(self, obs_size: int, n_actions: int, config: AgentConfig, *, seed: int) -> None: ...
214	    def values(self, obs: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray: ...
215	    @classmethod
216	    def load(cls, path: Path, env: Env) -> "DQNAgent": ...
217	```
218	- **Invariants:** `kind = "choices"`; `values` returns `n_actions` floats (AGENT-01); with `explore=False` it picks the highest value, ties going to the lowest index (AGENT-03); ε falls linearly from `eps_start` to `eps_end` over games `[0, n_games / 2)`, then stays at `eps_end` (AGENT-02); each `learn` remembers that agent's step and makes one update, targets `r + gamma · max Q_frozen(s′)` or `r` when done, the frozen copy refreshed every `refresh_every` updates (AGENT-04, AGENT-05, TWIN-02); `end_game` returns `None`; memory off learns from the latest transition only (ABL-01), frozen copy off uses the current network (ABL-02); a loaded agent picks the same moves (AGENT-06)
219	- **Errors:** `load` raises `ValueError` naming both observation sizes and move counts on a mismatch; `learn` raises `core.NonFiniteLossError` on a NaN or infinite loss or value
220	- **Boundary:** train, registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/agent.DQNAgent/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a recorded 3-game corridor training run; a saved full agent loaded back
221	- **Used by:** registry; PL-01, PL-04, PL-05
222	
223	## pg
224	
225	### pg.PGConfig: type
226	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class PGConfig: rung: Literal[1, 2, 3] = 3; value: bool = True; cap: bool = True; rescale: bool = True; hidden: int = 64; lr: float = 3e-4; gamma: float = 0.99; lam: float = 0.95; passes: int = 10; batch: int = 64; clip: float = 0.2; log_std_init: float = -0.5`
227	- **Invariants:** rung 1 is plain policy gradient, 2 actor-critic, 3 the limited-update agent (AD-13); `value`, `cap` and `rescale` are the tank's switches (ABL-06–ABL-08) and only rung 3 has them
228	- **Errors:** `ValueError` naming the switch and the rung if a switch is off on rung 1 or 2 (ABL edge case)
229	- **Boundary:** runs, study, registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/pg.PGConfig/ · **Cases:** each rung's default; rung 3 with each switch off alone; rung 1 with `cap` off (refused)
230	- **Used by:** runs, study, registry
231	
232	### pg.Spread: type
233	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Spread: centre: np.ndarray; width: np.ndarray`
234	- **Invariants:** one entry per control; `width` > 0 is the standard deviation of a normal draw around `centre` (PG-01)
235	- **Boundary:** pg tests · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/pg.Spread/ · **Cases:** an untrained rung's spread on a tank observation
236	- **Used by:** pg tests
237	
238	### pg.PGAgent: Hand-written policy-gradient ladder
239	```python
240	class PGAgent(LearningAgent):
241	    def __init__(self, obs_size: int, controls: Controls, config: PGConfig, *, seed: int) -> None: ...
242	    def policy(self, obs: np.ndarray) -> Spread: ...
243	    def value(self, obs: np.ndarray) -> float: ...
244	    @classmethod
245	    def load(cls, path: Path, env: Env) -> "PGAgent": ...
246	```
247	- **Invariants:** `kind = "controls"`; training moves are drawn from `policy(obs)`, evaluation moves are its centre, and both are clipped to the limits before they are sent (PG-01–PG-03); `learn` stores the step and returns `None`; `end_game` learns once from every stored step of every fish, then clears them (PG-04, PG-05), as AD-13 sets per rung and switch (PG-06–PG-09, ABL-06–ABL-08); a loaded agent picks the same moves (PG-12)
248	- **Errors:** `value` raises `ValueError` on a rung with no value estimate; `load` raises `ValueError` naming both observation sizes and controls on a mismatch; `end_game` raises `core.NonFiniteLossError`
249	- **Boundary:** train, registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/pg.PGAgent/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a recorded 3-game line run of each rung; a saved rung 3 loaded back
250	- **Used by:** registry; PL-01, PL-04, PL-05
251	
252	### pg.draw: A training draw
253	`def draw(spread: Spread, rng: np.random.Generator) -> np.ndarray`
254	- **Invariants:** one float64 entry per control, `centre + width · z` with `z` standard normal from `rng`; not clipped (PG-01); the same `rng` state gives the same draw (AD-18)
255	- **Used by:** `pg.PGAgent`; pg tests (TC-22)
256	
257	### pg.clip_move: A move clipped to the limits
258	`def clip_move(move: np.ndarray, controls: Controls) -> np.ndarray`
259	- **Invariants:** element-wise `min(max(move, low), high)`; a move already within the limits is returned unchanged (PG-03)
260	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `move`'s length isn't `len(controls.names)`
261	- **Used by:** `pg.PGAgent`; pg tests (TC-22)
262	
263	### pg.discounted_returns: One fish's returns
264	`def discounted_returns(rewards: Sequence[float], *, gamma: float = 0.99) -> np.ndarray`
265	- **Invariants:** one float64 entry per step, `G_t = r_t + gamma · G_{t+1}`, with nothing carried past the fish's last step, whether it was removed or reached the time limit (PG-05, PG-06, AD-13)
266	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `rewards` is empty
267	- **Used by:** `pg.step_weights`; pg tests (TC-24)
268	
269	### pg.advantages: One fish's blended advantages
270	`def advantages(rewards: Sequence[float], values: Sequence[float], *, gamma: float = 0.99, lam: float = 0.95) -> np.ndarray`
271	- **Invariants:** one float64 entry per step, the `lam`-blended sum of one-step errors `r_t + gamma · V_{t+1} − V_t`, with `V` after the fish's last step taken as 0 (PG-07, AD-13)
272	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if the two lengths differ or are 0
273	- **Used by:** `pg.step_weights`; pg tests (TC-24)
274	
275	### pg.rescale: Advantages rescaled over one game
276	`def rescale(weights: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray`
277	- **Invariants:** `(weights − mean) / (std + 1e-8)`, population standard deviation over every entry given; the input isn't changed (PG-09)
278	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `weights` is empty
279	- **Used by:** `pg.step_weights`; pg tests (TC-24)
280	
281	### pg.step_weights: The weight of every step in one game
282	`def step_weights(rewards: Mapping[int, Sequence[float]], values: Mapping[int, Sequence[float]] | None, config: PGConfig) -> dict[int, np.ndarray]`
283	- **Invariants:** per fish, `discounted_returns` on rung 1 and on rung 3 with `value` off, `advantages` otherwise; on rungs 2 and 3 with `rescale` on, every fish's entries are rescaled together as one game (PG-06, PG-07, PG-09, ABL-06, ABL-08)
284	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `values` is `None` where the config needs an estimate, or its fish or lengths differ from `rewards`
285	- **Used by:** `pg.PGAgent`; pg tests (TC-24)
286	
287	### pg.capped_objective: Each move's objective
288	`def capped_objective(ratio: torch.Tensor, advantage: torch.Tensor, *, clip: float | None = 0.2) -> torch.Tensor`
289	- **Invariants:** element-wise `min(ratio · advantage, clamp(ratio, 1 − clip, 1 + clip) · advantage)`, or `ratio · advantage` with `clip=None` (cap off, ABL-07); not averaged; differentiable in `ratio` (PG-08, AD-13)
290	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if the shapes differ or `clip` isn't in (0, 1)
291	- **Used by:** `pg.PGAgent`; pg tests (TC-25)
292	
293	## baselines
294	
295	### baselines.RandomAgent: Uniform random baseline
296	`class RandomAgent(Agent): name = "random"; kind = "any"`
297	- **Invariants:** picks uniformly from `range(n)` for `Choices`, and each control uniformly within its limits for `Controls` (BASE-01), with a generator reseeded by `reset(seed)`, so a replay on the same seed is identical (REPLAY-04)
298	- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.RandomAgent/ · **Cases:** a replay double of 10 moves on Snake seed 42; a replay double of 10 moves in the tank on seed 2
299	- **Used by:** registry; PL-02, PL-05
300	
301	### baselines.ScriptedAgent: Greedy, danger-avoiding Snake baseline
302	`class ScriptedAgent(Agent): name = "scripted"; kind = "choices"`
303	- **Invariants:** reads `env.state`; steps toward the food along the axis with the larger gap, and if that move is immediately fatal takes a non-fatal move, preferring straight; if every move is fatal it goes straight (BASE-02); deterministic
304	- **Errors:** `TypeError` naming the environment if `env` isn't a `SnakeEnv`
305	- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.ScriptedAgent/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a Snake seed 42 game; food ahead with a wall ahead
306	- **Used by:** registry; PL-02, PL-05
307	
308	### baselines.ScriptedFish: Ball-fleeing tank baseline
309	`class ScriptedFish(Agent): name = "scripted"; kind = "controls"`
310	- **Invariants:** reads only the observation, in `tank.TankEnv`'s layout; always speed 0.3; with the wall within 1 unit ahead (forward wall reading ≤ 0.25), turns 15° toward the side whose rays read more room, left winning a tie; else away from the ray seeing the ball closest: rays 1–3 → −15°, rays 5–7 → +15°, ray 0 → toward the side with more room, ray 4 → straight; on equal ball readings the further-left ray counts; no ball in reach → straight (BASE-04); deterministic
311	- **Errors:** `TypeError` naming the environment if it isn't the continuous tank
312	- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.ScriptedFish/ · **Cases:** the ball on the front-left ray only; no ball and the wall 0.5 ahead with more room on the left; the ball and the wall ahead at once
313	- **Used by:** registry; PL-02, PL-05
314	
315	## evaluate
316	
317	### evaluate.EVAL_SEEDS: Held-out seeds
318	`EVAL_SEEDS: tuple[int, ...] = tuple(range(1_000_000, 1_000_100))`
319	- **Invariants:** 100 seeds; disjoint from every training seed (EVAL-01); regular evaluation points use the first `RunConfig.eval_games` of them (EVAL-02)
320	- **Boundary:** train, study, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.EVAL_SEEDS/ · **Cases:** the 100-seed tuple
321	- **Used by:** train, study, replay
322	
323	### evaluate.Seeds: type
324	`Seeds: TypeAlias = tuple[int, ...]`
325	- **Invariants:** game seeds in the order the games are played; every seed is in `[0, 1_000_000)`, so none is a held-out seed (EVAL-01)
326	- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.Seeds/ · **Cases:** the 1,000 training seeds of run seed 1; the 200 training seeds of run seed 2
327	- **Used by:** train
328	
329	### evaluate.training_seeds: Training game seeds
330	`def training_seeds(run_seed: int, n_games: int) -> Seeds`
331	- **Invariants:** `n_games` seeds in `[0, 1_000_000)`, the same for the same `run_seed`
332	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `n_games` < 1
333	- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.training_seeds/ · **Cases:** seed 1 with 1,000 games
334	- **Used by:** train; PL-01
335	
336	### evaluate.GameResult: type
337	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class GameResult: seed: int; score: float; length: int; frames: tuple[Frame, ...]`
338	- **Invariants:** `length` is the number of environment steps; `frames` is empty unless requested, otherwise `length + 1` frames from the first to the last
339	- **Boundary:** train, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.GameResult/ · **Cases:** a corridor game with frames; a Snake game without frames; a tank game with frames
340	- **Used by:** train, replay
341	
342	### evaluate.EvalResult: type
343	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class EvalResult: seeds: tuple[int, ...]; scores: tuple[float, ...]; mean: float; std: float`
344	- **Invariants:** one score per seed, in seed order; `std` is the population standard deviation
345	- **Boundary:** runs, train, study · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.EvalResult/ · **Cases:** the random agent on 100 held-out Snake seeds; a 10-seed corridor evaluation; the scripted fish on the first 20 held-out tank seeds
346	- **Used by:** runs, train, study
347	
348	### evaluate.play_game: Play one game
349	`def play_game(env: Env, agent: Agent, seed: int, *, explore: bool = False, frames: bool = False) -> GameResult`
350	- **Invariants:** calls `env.reset(seed)` and `agent.reset(seed)`, then each step asks every living agent, in id order, for a move and stops asking a removed one (CORE-08), until done; the same inputs give the same result
351	- **Errors:** `InvalidMoveError` naming the agent, the move and the step number if a move is outside `env.moves` (CORE edge case)
352	- **Boundary:** train, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.play_game/ · **Cases:** the scripted agent on Snake seed 42 with frames; an agent returning move 5; the random agent on line seed 3
353	- **Used by:** train, replay, evaluate.evaluate; PL-01, PL-05
354	
355	### evaluate.evaluate: Greedy score on a seed set
356	`def evaluate(env: Env, agent: Agent, seeds: Sequence[int] = EVAL_SEEDS) -> EvalResult`
357	- **Invariants:** one `play_game(..., explore=False)` per seed; changes no agent state (EVAL-02, BASE-03)
358	- **Errors:** `MoveKindError` from `check_agent` before any game
359	- **Boundary:** train, study · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.evaluate/ · **Cases:** the random agent on the 100 held-out Snake seeds
360	- **Used by:** train, study; PL-01, PL-02
361	
362	## runs
363	
364	### runs.RunConfig: type
365	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class RunConfig: name: str; env: str; agent_kind: Literal["dqn", "pg", "random", "scripted"]; agent: AgentConfig | PGConfig | None; seed: int; n_games: int = 1_000; eval_every: int = 50; eval_games: int = 100; env_options: tuple[tuple[str, int], ...] = ()`
366	- **Invariants:** `agent` is an `AgentConfig` exactly when `agent_kind == "dqn"`, a `PGConfig` exactly when `"pg"`, else `None`; baseline runs have `n_games == 0`; `eval_games` ≤ 100; `env_options` are `make_env`'s keyword arguments (tank: `fish`, `compass`); `name` is a valid folder name (LOG-01)
367	- **Boundary:** train, study, registry, curves · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.RunConfig/ · **Cases:** the full Snake agent, seed 1; the memory-off agent, seed 3; the scripted baseline; rung 3 with the cap off in a 5-fish tank, `eval_every` 100 and `eval_games` 20
368	- **Used by:** train, study, registry, curves
369	
370	### runs.RunRecord: type
371	```python
372	@dataclass(frozen=True)
373	class RunRecord:
374	    config: RunConfig; status: Literal["running", "finished", "failed"]; error: str | None; elapsed_s: float
375	    games: tuple[GameStat, ...]; evals: tuple[EvalPoint, ...]; losses: tuple[LossPoint, ...]; final: EvalResult | None
376	```
377	- **Invariants:** `games[i]` is training game i (LOG-02); an evaluation and a loss point every `eval_every` games (LOG-03); `final` is over all `EVAL_SEEDS` and set exactly when `status == "finished"` (EVAL-02, AD-15); `error` names the game and step when `status == "failed"`
378	- **Boundary:** curves, study, registry, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.RunRecord/ · **Cases:** a finished 200-game corridor run; a Snake run failed at game 430 (evaluation points to game 400); a baseline record; a record with no evaluation points; a finished tank run with 10 points over 20 seeds and a final over 100
379	- **Used by:** curves, study, registry
380	
381	### runs.GameStat: type
382	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class GameStat: score: float; length: int`
383	- **Invariants:** `length` ≥ 1
384	- **Boundary:** curves, study · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.GameStat/ · **Cases:** a Snake game scoring 4 in 212 steps; a tank game scoring 550 in 1,000 steps
385	- **Used by:** curves, study
386	
387	### runs.EvalPoint: type
388	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class EvalPoint: game: int; result: EvalResult`
389	- **Invariants:** `game` is the number of training games played before this evaluation, a multiple of `eval_every`
390	- **Boundary:** curves, study · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.EvalPoint/ · **Cases:** the evaluation after game 50
391	- **Used by:** curves, study
392	
393	### runs.LossPoint: type
394	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class LossPoint: game: int; mean_loss: float`
395	- **Invariants:** the mean of the losses `learn` and `end_game` returned over the last `eval_every` games; NaN only if it never learned in that window
396	- **Boundary:** curves, study · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.LossPoint/ · **Cases:** the loss after game 100
397	- **Used by:** curves, study
398	
399	### runs.RunExistsError: type
400	`class RunExistsError(FileExistsError): name: str`
401	- **Invariants:** the message names the existing run and its environment
402	- **Boundary:** train, study, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.RunExistsError/ · **Cases:** creating `snake/full-s1` twice
403	- **Used by:** train, study, cli
404	
405	### runs.RunWriter: type
406	```python
407	class RunWriter(Protocol):
408	    path: Path
409	    def add_game(self, score: float, length: int) -> None: ...
410	    def add_eval(self, game: int, result: EvalResult) -> None: ...  # also flushes
411	    def add_loss(self, game: int, mean_loss: float) -> None: ...
412	    def save_agent(self, agent: LearningAgent) -> Path: ...
413	    def finish(self, status: Literal["finished", "failed"], error: str | None = None, final: EvalResult | None = None) -> RunRecord: ...
414	```
415	- **Invariants:** `record.json` is atomically rewritten after every `add_eval` and in `finish` (LOG-05); `save_agent` writes `agent.pt` next to the record (LOG-04); `finish("finished")` requires `final`
416	- **Boundary:** train, study · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.RunWriter/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a recorded 100-game run with 2 evaluation points and a final
417	- **Used by:** train, study; PL-01
418	
419	### runs.create_run: Start a run record
420	`def create_run(config: RunConfig, root: Path = Path("runs")) -> RunWriter`
421	- **Invariants:** creates `root/config.env/config.name/` and writes the config to the record at once (LOG-01, AD-15)
422	- **Errors:** `RunExistsError` naming the existing run if the folder exists; nothing is overwritten
423	- **Boundary:** train, study · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.create_run/ · **Cases:** a new corridor run; an existing run name
424	- **Used by:** train, study; PL-01, PL-02
425	
426	### runs.load_record: Read a run record
427	`def load_record(path: Path) -> RunRecord`
428	- **Errors:** `FileNotFoundError` naming the run if `record.json` is missing
429	- **Boundary:** curves, study, registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.load_record/ · **Cases:** a finished corridor record; a record failed at game 430
430	- **Used by:** curves, study, registry; PL-02, PL-03, PL-04, PL-05
431	
432	## train
433	
434	### train.train_run: Train one agent on one environment
435	`def train_run(env: Env, agent: LearningAgent, config: RunConfig, *, root: Path = Path("runs")) -> RunRecord`
436	- **Invariants:** checks `env` and `agent` first; plays `config.n_games` games on `training_seeds(config.seed, n_games)`: `begin_game`, then each step `act(explore=True)` per living agent in id order, `env.step`, `learn` per agent's `Transition` in id order, and `end_game` after the game (AD-12); every `eval_every` games records `evaluate` on the first `eval_games` held-out seeds and the mean loss; finishes with `final` over all `EVAL_SEEDS`, reusing the last point when it already is one, and saves the agent; same config → same record apart from `elapsed_s` (QR-05); works for any `Env` (CORE-05, CORE-07)
437	- **Errors:** `EnvCheckError`, `MoveKindError` and `RunExistsError` before any game; on any later failure, including `NonFiniteLossError` and `InvalidMoveError`, finishes the record as `failed`, naming the game and step, then re-raises
438	- **Boundary:** study, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/train.train_run/ · **Cases:** a 200-game corridor run, seed 1; a run that hits a non-finite loss; a 100-game line run of rung 1
439	- **Used by:** study, cli; PL-01, PL-04
440	
441	## registry
442	
443	### registry.make_env: Environment by name
444	`def make_env(name: str, **options: int) -> Env`
445	- **Invariants:** `"snake"` → `SnakeEnv()`, `"corridor"` → `CorridorEnv()`, `"tank"` → `TankEnv(fish=options.get("fish", 5), compass=bool(options.get("compass", 0)))`, `"line"` → `LineEnv()`; the result passes `check_env`
446	- **Errors:** `KeyError` naming the known environments; `TypeError` naming an option the environment doesn't take
447	- **Boundary:** study, replay, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.make_env/ · **Cases:** `snake`; `corridor`; `tank` with 3 fish; `tank` with `compass=1`; an unknown name
448	- **Used by:** study, replay, cli; PL-01, PL-02, PL-04, PL-05
449	
450	### registry.EvalSchedule: type
451	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class EvalSchedule: every: int; games: int`
452	- **Invariants:** `every` ≥ 1; 1 ≤ `games` ≤ 100; fills `RunConfig.eval_every` and `RunConfig.eval_games` (AD-15)
453	- **Boundary:** study, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.EvalSchedule/ · **Cases:** Snake's `(50, 100)`; the tank's `(100, 20)` for 1,000 games; the tank's `(1, 20)` for 5 games
454	- **Used by:** study, cli; PL-01, PL-04
455	
456	### registry.eval_schedule: Evaluation cadence per environment
457	`def eval_schedule(env_name: str, n_games: int) -> EvalSchedule`
458	- **Invariants:** `EvalSchedule(50, 100)` for `snake` and `corridor`; `EvalSchedule(max(1, n_games // 10), 20)` for `tank` and `line` (EVAL-02, AD-15)
459	- **Errors:** `KeyError` naming the known environments
460	- **Boundary:** study, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.eval_schedule/ · **Cases:** snake with 1,000 games; tank with 1,000 games; tank with 5 games
461	- **Used by:** study, cli; PL-01, PL-04
462	
463	### registry.make_agent: Learning agent for a run
464	`def make_agent(config: RunConfig, env: Env) -> LearningAgent`
465	- **Invariants:** `"dqn"` → `DQNAgent(env.obs_size, env.moves.n, config.agent, seed=config.seed)`; `"pg"` → `PGAgent(env.obs_size, env.moves, config.agent, seed=config.seed)`; the result passes `check_agent` with `env`
466	- **Errors:** `MoveKindError` naming both kinds (the DQN on the continuous tank, a rung on Snake); `ValueError` for a baseline `agent_kind`
467	- **Boundary:** study, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.make_agent/ · **Cases:** the full DQN on Snake; rung 3 on the tank; the DQN on the compass tank; the DQN on the continuous tank (refused)
468	- **Used by:** study, cli; PL-01, PL-04
469	
470	### registry.load_agent: Agent by name or run folder
471	`def load_agent(spec: str, env: Env) -> Agent`
472	- **Invariants:** `"random"` gives `RandomAgent`; `"scripted"` gives `ScriptedAgent` on Snake and `ScriptedFish` on the continuous tank (AD-08); any other `spec` is a run folder whose record's `agent_kind` picks `DQNAgent.load` or `PGAgent.load` for its `agent.pt`
473	- **Errors:** `FileNotFoundError` naming the run if it doesn't exist or has no saved agent; `ValueError` naming the environment if `"scripted"` has none there
474	- **Boundary:** replay, study, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.load_agent/ · **Cases:** `random`; `scripted` on snake; `scripted` on the tank; a saved DQN run folder; a saved rung 3 run folder; a missing run
475	- **Used by:** replay, study, cli; PL-02, PL-05
476	
477	## curves
478	
479	### curves.CurvePlot: type
480	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class CurvePlot: path: Path; lines: tuple[str, ...]; skipped: tuple[str, ...]`
481	- **Invariants:** `lines` are the drawn labels, baselines included; `skipped` names every record with no evaluation points
482	- **Boundary:** study, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/curves.CurvePlot/ · **Cases:** the full agent with 3 ablations and 2 baselines; one record skipped; the 3 rungs with 2 baselines
483	- **Used by:** study, cli
484	
485	### curves.plot_curves: Learning-curve chart
486	`def plot_curves(records: Sequence[RunRecord], out: Path, *, baselines: Sequence[RunRecord] = ()) -> CurvePlot`
487	- **Invariants:** one line per config minus seed: the mean evaluation score at each evaluation point, with a min–max band across seeds (CURVE-01, CURVE-02); each baseline as a labelled flat line (CURVE-03); labels name the agent and each switch that is off: `full` or the DQN's switches off (CURVE-04), a rung's name (`reinforce`, `actor-critic`, `ppo`) plus any switch off (CURVE-06), `compass` added for the compass tank; reads only records, in ≤ 10 s (CURVE-05)
488	- **Errors:** `ValueError` naming the environments if the records come from more than one
489	- **Boundary:** study, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/curves.plot_curves/ · **Cases:** the full agent, 3 ablations and 2 baselines; 5 seeds of each rung and 2 tank baselines; records from two environments
490	- **Used by:** study, cli; PL-03, PL-04
491	
492	## replay
493	
494	### replay.Replay: type
495	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Replay: agent: str; env: str; seed: int; frames: tuple[Frame, ...]; score: float`
496	- **Invariants:** `frames` runs from the first frame to game over; `score == frames[-1].score`
497	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.Replay/ · **Cases:** the scripted agent on Snake seed 42; the random agent on corridor seed 3; the scripted fish on tank seed 2
498	- **Used by:** cli
499	
500	### replay.record_replay: Re-play a game
501	`def record_replay(agent_spec: str, env_name: str, seed: int, **options: int) -> Replay`
502	- **Invariants:** `make_env(env_name, **options)`, taking a run folder's own `env_options` when none are given, then `load_agent` and `play_game(..., explore=False, frames=True)`; the same inputs give identical frames (REPLAY-04); on a held-out seed the score equals the logged evaluation score (REPLAY-05)
503	- **Errors:** as `registry.load_agent` and `registry.make_env`, raised before any window opens
504	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.record_replay/ · **Cases:** the scripted agent on Snake seed 42; a saved rung on tank seed 1,000,000
505	- **Used by:** cli; PL-05
506	
507	### replay.Playback: type
508	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Playback: frames: tuple[int, ...]; fps: tuple[int, ...]`
509	- **Invariants:** one entry per frame shown, in order: `frames[i]` indexes `Replay.frames` and `fps[i]` is the speed when it was shown, within 1–30; the two have the same length; a pause adds no entry, and a step while paused adds exactly the next index (REPLAY-03)
510	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.Playback/ · **Cases:** a 3-frame replay played through at 10 fps; paused after frame 0 then stepped once; sped up to 30 fps after frame 1
511	- **Used by:** cli
512	
513	### replay.show: Replay window
514	`def show(replay: Replay, *, fps: int = 10) -> Playback`
515	- **Invariants:** draws each frame's cells, then its shapes, with the step, score and caption (REPLAY-02); space pauses, → steps exactly one frame while paused, ↑/↓ change the speed within 1–30 fps (REPLAY-03); stays on the last frame until closed, then returns what it showed; reads keys only from the pygame event queue, so a test drives it headless with SDL's dummy video driver and posted key events
516	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `fps` is outside 1–30
517	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.show/ · **Cases:** a 3-frame corridor replay shown headless; a 3-frame tank replay shown headless
518	- **Used by:** cli; PL-05
519	
520	## study
521	
522	### study.StudyPlan: type
523	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class StudyPlan: env: str; full: str; learners: tuple[str, ...]; ablations: tuple[str, ...]; configs: dict[str, AgentConfig | PGConfig]; env_options: tuple[tuple[str, int], ...]`
524	- **Invariants:** `configs` has one entry per variant name; `full` is in `learners`, the agents compared with both baselines, listed in rung order, so a plan with several learners is a ladder (AD-16); `ablations` are compared with `full`; `STUDIES["snake"]` is `full` / (`full`,) / (`no-memory`, `no-frozen`, `no-decay`); `STUDIES["tank"]` is `ppo` / (`reinforce`, `actor-critic`, `ppo`) / (`no-value`, `no-cap`, `no-rescale`) with 5 fish
525	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/study.StudyPlan/ · **Cases:** `STUDIES["snake"]`; `STUDIES["tank"]`
526	- **Used by:** study.run_study, study.check_notes; PL-04, PL-06
527	
528	### study.Comparison: type
529	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Comparison: label: str; verdict: Literal["beats", "does not beat", "better", "hurts learning", "no detectable difference", "missing"]; method: str; statistic: float | None; p_value: float | None; p_adjusted: float | None`
530	- **Invariants:** baseline comparisons use "beats", "does not beat" or "missing" (EVAL-03); ladder comparisons "better", "no detectable difference" or "missing" (PG-10); ablation comparisons "hurts learning", "no detectable difference" or "missing" (ABL-04); "missing" has no statistics
531	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/study.Comparison/ · **Cases:** the agent beats the random baseline; an ablation with no detectable difference; a missing ablation; rung 3 better than rung 2
532	- **Used by:** cli
533	
534	### study.compare_scores: One-sided comparison (null-hypothesis-testing)
535	`def compare_scores(full: np.ndarray, other: np.ndarray, *, label: str, n_comparisons: int, alpha: float = 0.05) -> Comparison`
536	- **Invariants:** `full` and `other` are each k × 100 (finished training seed × held-out seed, 1 ≤ k ≤ 5; a baseline is 1 × 100), columns in `EVAL_SEEDS` order; pairs by held-out game on each side's per-game mean over its rows; tests whether `full` is higher, corrected for `n_comparisons`; `method` states the seeds each side used (AD-09); the test is built with the **`null-hypothesis-testing`** skill
537	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if the column counts differ, or if either side has no rows or more than 5
538	- **Used by:** study.run_study; PL-04
539	
540	### study.StudyReport: type
541	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class StudyReport: env: str; baselines: tuple[Comparison, ...]; ladder: tuple[Comparison, ...]; ablations: tuple[Comparison, ...]; spreads: dict[str, float]; incomplete: tuple[str, ...]; report: Path; charts: tuple[Path, ...]`
542	- **Invariants:** 2 baseline comparisons per learner, one ladder comparison per rung above the first, 3 ablation comparisons (Snake: 2, 0, 3; tank: 6, 2, 3); `spreads` is each learner's standard deviation of its per-seed mean final score (PG-10); `incomplete` names runs left out (EVAL edge case); when no ablation hurts learning, or a rung isn't better, the report states it as a finding and the study still succeeds (ABL-05, PG-11); `charts` is `curves.png`, plus `ladder.png` when there's a ladder
543	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/study.StudyReport/ · **Cases:** a complete Snake study; a study where all 3 ablations show no detectable difference; a study with a failed ablation run; a tank study where rung 3 shows no detectable difference
544	- **Used by:** cli
545	
546	### study.run_study: The full study
547	`def run_study(env_name: str = "snake", seeds: Sequence[int] = (1, 2, 3, 4, 5), *, n_games: int = 1_000, workers: int | None = None, root: Path = Path("runs")) -> StudyReport`
548	- **Invariants:** trains every variant of `STUDIES[env_name]` for each seed in parallel processes, on `registry.eval_schedule`'s cadence; scores both baselines on `EVAL_SEEDS`; compares on each run's `final`, correcting within each family (baselines: 2 per learner; ladder: rungs − 1; ablations: 3; AD-16); writes `root/<env>/study/report.md` and its charts; runs unattended (QR-02)
549	- **Errors:** a failed run is reported as missing or incomplete, never as a difference: a learner's unfinished runs are left out of its baseline comparisons, which use its finished ones; an ablation, or a rung in a ladder comparison, with any unfinished run is `missing` (AD-09); `RuntimeError` naming the runs only if `full` has no finished run
550	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/study.run_study/ · **Cases:** a 2-seed, 100-game corridor study with one ablation run failing; the same study with one full-agent run failing; a 2-seed, 20-game line study of the 3 rungs
551	- **Used by:** cli; PL-04
552	
553	### study.NotesCheck: type
554	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class NotesCheck: page: Path; exists: bool; missing_sections: tuple[str, ...]; empty_sections: tuple[str, ...]; broken_links: tuple[str, ...]; ok: bool`
555	- **Invariants:** required sections, from the environment's `StudyPlan` so a page checked before any study still gets a section check, are "Why it learns" (EXPL-01), one per ablation under its variant name (EXPL-02) and one per learner after the first under its rung name (EXPL-04); `broken_links` names each of `runs/<env>/study/report.md` and its charts that the page doesn't link or whose link doesn't resolve from the page's folder (EXPL-03); when `exists` is false every required section is missing; `ok` is true exactly when the page exists and all three tuples are empty
556	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/study.NotesCheck/ · **Cases:** a complete Snake page; a missing page; a page with an empty "Why it learns" section and no chart link; a tank page missing its `ppo` section
557	- **Used by:** cli; PL-06
558	
559	### study.check_notes: Check the learner's notes page
560	`def check_notes(env_name: str, *, notes_root: Path, runs_root: Path) -> NotesCheck`
561	- **Invariants:** reads `notes_root/<env>.md` and `runs_root/<env>/study/report.md` only; a section is a Markdown heading of any level, matched case-insensitively, and is empty when no non-blank text comes before the next heading of the same or a higher level; never writes, creates or changes a file, so the page is byte-identical afterwards (AD-10)
562	- **Errors:** `KeyError` naming the studied environments for an environment with no `StudyPlan`; none for a missing page or a missing report, which show up in the result
563	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/study.check_notes/ · **Cases:** a complete Snake page beside a study folder; no page; a page whose chart link points to a missing file; a complete tank page
564	- **Used by:** cli; PL-06

# Idea: Predator-Prey Foraging

**Status:** Specced · **Last updated:** 2026-10-19 · **Brief:** [../brief.md](../brief.md)
**Spec:** HUNT-01–HUNT-12, SELF-01–SELF-08, CROSS-01–CROSS-04, CORE-10, BASE-05–BASE-06, CURVE-07, EXPL-05; CORE-02–CORE-04, CORE-09, PG-04, EVAL-02–EVAL-04, LOG-01, LOG-02, LOG-04, CURVE-03, REPLAY-01, REPLAY-02, EXPL-03, QR-01–QR-03 (changed)

> Record of why, frozen when specced. The spec is current: [spec.md](../spec.md)

## In one sentence
This adds a third environment, the fish tank with food pellets and a learning predator, so the learner can see co-evolution: two sides that learn against each other, why that breaks plain training, and how training against past versions fixes it.

## The problem
In Snake and the tank the world stands still while the agent learns. When the opponent also learns, the target keeps moving: each side's reward can stay flat while both improve, and the two can chase each other in circles, forgetting old tricks as they learn new ones. Self-play against past versions is the usual fix. Building the broken version first, then the fix, shows why the fix exists.

## Who it's for
The same learner as the brief, once the fish tank (S2) is built and they move on from one learner in a fixed world to two learners shaping each other's world.

## How it fits
- **Serves:** Goal 1 (explain why self-play works), Goal 2 (beats random and scripted opponents), Goal 3 (replay), Goal 4 (reuses the shared core and S2's tank and PPO)
- **Touches:** S2's tank (TANK: the ball becomes a predator, pellets are added, prey have energy); PG-08 (PPO, reused for both sides); CORE-02, CORE-05, CORE-07 and CORE-08 (one trained agent per run becomes two learners with their own policies in one game); BASE, EVAL, CURVE, REPLAY, EXPL (written for one learner against a fixed world). The spec's "Fish that affect each other" and "Real environments beyond the fish tank" Out of scope items narrow. The frozen [fish-tank.md](fish-tank.md) stays as it is.

1	# Idea: Predator-Prey Foraging
2	
3	**Status:** Specced · **Last updated:** 2026-10-19 · **Brief:** [../brief.md](../brief.md)
4	**Spec:** HUNT-01–HUNT-12, SELF-01–SELF-08, CROSS-01–CROSS-04, CORE-10, BASE-05–BASE-06, CURVE-07, EXPL-05; CORE-02–CORE-04, CORE-09, PG-04, EVAL-02–EVAL-04, LOG-01, LOG-02, LOG-04, CURVE-03, REPLAY-01, REPLAY-02, EXPL-03, QR-01–QR-03 (changed)

6	> Record of why, frozen when specced. The spec is current: [spec.md](../spec.md)
7	
8	## In one sentence
9	This adds a third environment, the fish tank with food pellets and a learning predator, so the learner can see co-evolution: two sides that learn against each other, why that breaks plain training, and how training against past versions fixes it.
10	
11	## The problem
12	In Snake and the tank the world stands still while the agent learns. When the opponent also learns, the target keeps moving: each side's reward can stay flat while both improve, and the two can chase each other in circles, forgetting old tricks as they learn new ones. Self-play against past versions is the usual fix. Building the broken version first, then the fix, shows why the fix exists.
13	
14	## Who it's for
15	The same learner as the brief, once the fish tank (S2) is built and they move on from one learner in a fixed world to two learners shaping each other's world.
16	
17	## How it fits
18	- **Serves:** Goal 1 (explain why self-play works), Goal 2 (beats random and scripted opponents), Goal 3 (replay), Goal 4 (reuses the shared core and S2's tank and PPO)
19	- **Touches:** S2's tank (TANK: the ball becomes a predator, pellets are added, prey have energy); PG-08 (PPO, reused for both sides); CORE-02, CORE-05, CORE-07 and CORE-08 (one trained agent per run becomes two learners with their own policies in one game); BASE, EVAL, CURVE, REPLAY, EXPL (written for one learner against a fixed world). The spec's "Fish that affect each other" and "Real environments beyond the fish tank" Out of scope items narrow. The frozen [fish-tank.md](fish-tank.md) stays as it is.
20	- **Brief changes:** Not doing narrowed: predator-prey foraging is planned now; only environments after it wait.
21	- **Stage:** New S3 (roadmap stage on acceptance), built after S2 is done.
22	
23	## Recommended direction
24	**The tank, with stakes.** S2's circular tank, with the bouncing ball replaced by one predator fish, and food pellets scattered around. Prey lose energy over time and must eat pellets to keep it up, so a prey that only hides starves. A prey caught by the predator, or out of energy, is removed. The predator is rewarded for catching prey; prey are rewarded for staying alive. Both sides see only nearby things through rays, as in S2.
25	
26	**One brain per side.** All prey share one policy, as the fish do in S2. There is one predator with its own policy. Both are the learner's hand-written PPO from S2.
27	
28	**The ladder.**
29	1. *Taking turns* (optional first rung): one side trains while the other is frozen, then they swap. The world is still for each learner, so S2's PPO works unchanged.
30	2. *Simultaneous learners*: both sides learn at once. This is the broken version, kept on purpose: it shows the moving target and the cycling.
31	3. *Self-play against past versions*: each side saves a copy of itself as a new generation every so often, and trains against a mix of its opponent's current and past generations. This is the fix.
32	
33	**Judging it.** Each side's reward alone can't show learning, because the opponent improves too. Two fixed bars do: each side is scored against a random and a scripted opponent on the same seeded games. And a cross-play table pits every saved generation of one side against every generation of the other, so later generations beating earlier ones shows real progress rather than circling.
34	
35	**Time.** The brief's limit of about an hour of training covers the whole self-play run, every generation of both sides, not each generation.
36	
37	**The core grows again.** The shared core learns to run two learners with their own policies in one game. Snake and the tank keep running as before.
38	
39	## Alternatives considered
40	- Evolutionary methods (a population, keep the fittest) — a different lesson from the policy gradients the lab has built up to.
41	- Simultaneous learners only — shows the problem without the fix.
42	- Pellets only as a bonus reward — prey can win by hiding forever, so there is no trade-off to learn.
43	- Several predators — a second multi-agent problem (teamwork) on top of the first.
44	
45	## What success looks like
46	- Each side's learning curve beats a random and a scripted opponent on the same seeded games.
47	- In the cross-play table, later generations beat earlier ones, for both sides.
48	- Simultaneous learners visibly cycle or stall where self-play does not, or the notes record that they didn't.
49	- A replay shows prey foraging and dodging while the predator hunts.
50	- Snake's and the tank's results are unchanged after the core grows.
51	
52	## What we're betting on
53	- S2's rays and PPO carry over to both sides with small changes.
54	- Simultaneous learners show the moving-target failure clearly in a small tank.
55	- A whole self-play run, over a handful of generations, fits in about an hour on a laptop CPU.
56	
57	## Not doing
58	- Evolutionary methods — a different lesson.
59	- More than one predator — teamwork is a separate problem.
60	- Learned communication or teaming among prey — the prey share a policy, nothing more.
61	
62	## Open questions
63	Resolved in the spec.

156	# RL Lab
157	
158	**Status:** Accepted · **Last updated:** 2026-10-18
159	
160	## The problem
161	Reinforcement learning (RL) is how an agent learns by trial and error from rewards. It is easy to read about and hard to really understand. Library code hides the parts that matter, and tutorials stop once the agent "works". The fastest way to learn it is to build small agents from scratch, in small 2D game environments, where each one teaches one idea and you can see it learn.
162	
163	## Who it's for
164	Me: a solid Python programmer with some NumPy and PyTorch, who knows the basics of Q-learning (learning the value of each action in each state) and wants to learn RL by building it.
165	
166	## Goals
167	1. For each environment, I can explain in my own words why the agent learns, and why each part of the algorithm matters.
168	2. Every agent is shown to learn: its learning curve beats a random agent and a simple scripted agent on the same games.
169	3. I can watch a replay of any trained agent playing.
170	4. A new environment reuses a shared core (environment interface, training loop, logging, replay viewer, learning curves) instead of starting over.
171	
172	## Constraints
173	- Laptop CPU only, and under about an hour of training per environment.
174	- Free tools only.
175	- Evenings and weekends, a few weeks per environment, no deadline.
176	- Learning algorithms are written from scratch, not taken from a library.
177	
178	## What success looks like
179	- The first environment, Snake, has an agent whose learning curve clearly beats both baselines, plus a replay of it playing.
180	- Turning off a key part of the agent visibly hurts its learning, and I can say why.
181	- The second environment, whatever it is, plugs into the shared core with small changes.
182	
183	## What we're betting on
184	- Small, hand-picked descriptions of the game state ("danger ahead", "food to the left") are enough for an agent to learn well within an hour on a CPU.
185	- A shared core designed while Snake is the only example will still fit the next environment without a rewrite.
186	- Writing the algorithms myself teaches more than tuning a library would.
187	
188	## Not doing
189	- Learning from raw pixels or the full grid image: much slower, and not the lesson I'm after.
190	- Comparing against library implementations, for now: a later step, once my own version works.
191	- Fancy views such as heatmaps of the agent's values: learning curves and replays are enough.
192	- Planning environments beyond the third, predator-prey foraging: each next one is chosen once the one before is built.
193	- Mastering Snake: agents that see only nearby danger tend to trap themselves, and that limit is fine.
194	# Routed changes
195	
196	Changes a skill found that another skill owns, waiting for the owner's Revise. Written by `dt route`; the owner ticks its items (`dt route --done <skill>`) when the user approves that Revise.

# Architecture Example: Extend

The `plate-qc-ranking` idea was specced as `QCR-01`–`QCR-05` plus `DATA-07`. `dt status` reports those six as uncovered. The skill reads the idea file for context and runs the Grow check: gradient-boosted trees add one library and nothing runs or deploys on its own, so no trigger fires and it says so at the gate. It adds a `ranking` component covering QCR-01–QCR-04, with a `### ranking.rank_plates` section in `contract.md`. It adds QCR-05 (the review screen sort order) to the existing `review_ui` component's Covers, and records `AD-09: gradient-boosted trees for plate risk`, checked against the spec's baseline criterion (must beat sorting by Z′). One Changelog row, sourced from `ideas/plate-qc-ranking.md`. Status goes back to Draft.

# rllab — Pipelines

**Package:** rllab · **Status:** Approved · **Last updated:** 2026-10-18 · **Contract:** [contract.md](contract.md)

## PL-01: Train an agent
**Entry:** `src/rllab/cli/pipeline.py::run_train` · **Trigger:** `python -m rllab train --env snake --seed 1 [--agent dqn|pg] [--rung 1|2|3] [--no-memory] [--no-frozen] [--no-decay] [--no-value] [--no-cap] [--no-rescale] [--fish 5] [--compass] [--games 1000] [--name NAME]`
**Purpose:** Train one hand-written learner (the DQN, or a rung of the policy-gradient ladder) on an environment, with any of its parts switched off, and keep its record and trained agent in `runs/<env>/<name>/`.
**Covers:** CORE-01–CORE-09, SNAKE-01–SNAKE-08, TANK-01–TANK-10, TWIN-01, TWIN-02, AGENT-01–AGENT-06, PG-01–PG-09, PG-12, ABL-01–ABL-03, ABL-06–ABL-08, EVAL-01, EVAL-02, LOG-01–LOG-05, QR-01, QR-03–QR-05

| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `train_config` | cli.args | flags → RunConfig | `--agent dqn` switches into AgentConfig, `--agent pg` rung and switches into PGConfig; a Snake switch with `pg` or a tank switch with `dqn` refused, naming the switch and the agent; `--fish`/`--compass` into `env_options`; cadence from `eval_schedule`; default name `<variant>-s<seed>` | LOG-01, ABL-01–ABL-03, ABL-06–ABL-08 |
| 2 | `eval_schedule` | registry.schedule | env name, n games → EvalSchedule | plain match: `(50, 100)` for snake and corridor, `(max(1, n // 10), 20)` for tank and line (AD-15) | EVAL-02 |
| 3 | `make_env` | registry.envs | env name, options → Env | plain match on `snake`, `corridor`, `tank` or `line`, passing the tank's `fish` and `compass` | CORE-06, CORE-07 |
| 4 | `SnakeEnv` | snake.env | size → Env | 20 × 20 rules, 11 facts, rewards, step limit, frame; agent id 0 only | SNAKE-01–SNAKE-08, CORE-01–CORE-04 |
| 5 | `CorridorEnv` | corridor.env | length, max steps → Env | one-hot position, +1 at the right end; agent id 0 only | CORE-05, CORE-06 |
| 6 | `TankEnv` | tank.env | fish, compass → Env | NumPy rules in tank units (AD-14): turn then swim with a wall stop, the ball's mirror bounce, removal under 1.3, the 1,000-step limit, +1 per step survived; 8 rays cast for all living fish at once; frame with wall, ball and heading segments; `compass` swaps in `Choices(8)` at speed 0.3 | TANK-01–TANK-10, TWIN-01, CORE-02–CORE-04 |
| 7 | `LineEnv` | line.env | points, length, max steps → Env | each point moves by its push, held at 0; +1 and removed at `length`; ends when all are removed or at `max_steps` | CORE-07, CORE-08 |
| 8 | `make_agent` | registry.learners | RunConfig, Env → LearningAgent | plain match on `agent_kind`: `dqn` → `DQNAgent`, `pg` → `PGAgent`, then `check_agent` | CORE-07, ABL-01–ABL-03, ABL-06–ABL-08 |
| 9 | `DQNAgent` | agent.dqn | obs size, choice count, AgentConfig, seed → LearningAgent | 11 → 256 → 3 MLP learning in `learn` from the latest Transition only, ε fixed at 0.1, targets from the current net (every switch off); `end_game` returns `None`; `torch.save` state dict | AGENT-01–AGENT-06, ABL-01–ABL-03, TWIN-02, QR-03, QR-04 |
| 10 | `PGAgent` | pg.agent | obs size, Controls, PGConfig, seed → LearningAgent | rung 1 for every rung: obs → 64 → 64 → centres MLP with a learned log width, normal draws clipped to the limits, centre when greedy; `learn` stores the step; `end_game` weights each step's log-probability by the discounted return (0.99) to that fish's end and makes one Adam step; `torch.save` state dict (AD-13) | PG-01–PG-06, PG-12, QR-03 |
| 11 | `train_run` | train.loop | Env, LearningAgent, RunConfig → RunRecord | game loop: `act(explore=True)` per living agent in id order, `step`, `learn` per Transition in id order, `end_game` after each game (AD-12); evaluate on the first `eval_games` held-out seeds and log every `eval_every` games; the final check over all 100; failed record on any error | CORE-05, CORE-07–CORE-09, AGENT-02, EVAL-02, LOG-02, LOG-03, QR-01, QR-05 |
| 12 | `check_env` | core.check | Env → Env | `hasattr` on the 5 behaviours | CORE-05 |
| 13 | `check_agent` | core.check | Agent, Env → Agent | `agent.kind` against the kind of `env.moves`; returns the agent unchanged | CORE-07 |
| 14 | `training_seeds` | evaluate.seeds | run seed, n games → Seeds | generator from the run seed over `[0, 1_000_000)` (AD-04) | EVAL-01, QR-05 |
| 15 | `create_run` | runs.store | RunConfig → RunWriter | new `root/<env>/<name>/` folder, config written at once; refuses an existing name (AD-15) | LOG-01 |
| 16 | `JsonRunWriter` | runs.writer | RunConfig, path → RunWriter | record in memory, atomic rewrite on `add_eval` and `finish` (with `final`), `agent.pt` beside it (AD-05) | LOG-02–LOG-05 |
| 17 | `evaluate` | evaluate.score | Env, Agent, seeds → EvalResult | `check_agent`, then `play_game` greedily on each seed; mean and std | EVAL-02, AGENT-03, PG-02 |
| 18 | `play_game` | evaluate.play | Env, Agent, seed → GameResult | reset both; each step ask every living agent in id order, stop asking a removed one, until done | CORE-02, CORE-08, AGENT-03 |

### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
```python
def run_train(args: Namespace) -> RunRecord:
    config = train_config(args)
    env = make_env(config.env, **dict(config.env_options))
    agent = make_agent(config, env)
    return train_run(env, agent, config, root=args.root)
```
