# rllab — Contract

**Package:** rllab · **Status:** Draft · **Last updated:** 2026-10-19 · **Architecture:** [../../architecture.md](../../architecture.md)

## core

### core.Choices: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Choices: n: int`
- **Invariants:** `n` ≥ 1; a move is an `int` in `range(n)` (CORE-03)
- **Boundary:** snake, corridor, tank, agent, baselines, evaluate · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Choices/ · **Cases:** Snake's 3 moves; the compass tank's 8 moves
- **Used by:** snake, corridor, tank, agent, baselines, evaluate, registry

### core.Controls: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Controls: names: tuple[str, ...]; low: tuple[float, ...]; high: tuple[float, ...]`
- **Invariants:** the three tuples have the same length ≥ 1; `low[i] < high[i]`; a move is a float64 array of length `len(names)` with `low ≤ move ≤ high` element-wise (CORE-03)
- **Boundary:** tank, hunt, line, chase, pg, baselines, evaluate · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Controls/ · **Cases:** the tank's and the prey's `("turn", "speed")`, −15 to 15 degrees and 0 to 0.3; the predator's −10 to 10 degrees and 0 to 0.35; the line's `("push",)`, −1 to 1
- **Used by:** tank, hunt, line, chase, pg, baselines, evaluate, registry

### core.Moves: type
`Moves: TypeAlias = Choices | Controls`
- **Invariants:** every side reports exactly one (CORE-03)
- **Boundary:** every environment and agent · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Moves/ · **Cases:** `Choices(3)`; the tank's `Controls`
- **Used by:** core.Side, core.InvalidMoveError, registry

### core.Move: type
`Move: TypeAlias = int | np.ndarray`
- **Invariants:** an `int` for `Choices`; a float64 array shaped `(len(names),)` for `Controls`
- **Boundary:** every environment and agent · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Move/ · **Cases:** Snake move 2; tank move `[15.0, 0.3]`; predator move `[-10.0, 0.35]`
- **Used by:** core.Env, core.Agent, core.Transition, evaluate, train

### core.Side: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Side: name: str; obs_size: int; moves: Moves; ids: tuple[int, ...]`
- **Invariants:** `name` is a lowercase word, unique among the environment's sides; `ids` is non-empty, ascending and disjoint from every other side's; every observation of an id in `ids` has length `obs_size`, and its moves follow `moves` (CORE-03, AD-19)
- **Boundary:** every environment, agent, pg, baselines, evaluate, train, registry, crossplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Side/ · **Cases:** Snake's `Side("snake", 11, Choices(3), (0,))`; the 5-fish tank's `fish` side, ids 0–4; the hunt's `predator` side (17, the predator's controls, id 0) and `prey` side (34, the tank's controls, ids 1–5)
- **Used by:** core.Env, agent, pg, evaluate, train, registry, crossplay

### core.Scores: type
`Scores: TypeAlias = dict[str, float]`
- **Invariants:** one entry per side name: Snake food eaten; the tank's fish mean steps survived; the hunt's predator prey caught and prey mean steps survived (HUNT-10, HUNT-11); the line's points that reached the end; the corridor 1 if it reached the end; the chase's chaser 1 if it touched, runner steps untouched
- **Boundary:** every environment, evaluate, runs, replay, crossplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Scores/ · **Cases:** `{"snake": 4.0}`; `{"predator": 3.0, "prey": 550.0}`
- **Used by:** core.Step, core.Frame, evaluate, runs, replay

### core.Env: type
```python
class Env(Protocol):
    name: str; sides: tuple[Side, ...]
    def reset(self, seed: int) -> dict[int, np.ndarray]: ...
    def step(self, moves: Mapping[int, Move]) -> Step: ...
    def render(self) -> Frame: ...
```
- **Invariants:** `reset` returns one float32 observation per id of every side, of its side's `obs_size`; `step` takes one move per living id (CORE-02); the same seed and moves give identical steps and frames (CORE-01, CORE-02); Snake, the corridor, the tank and the line have one side, the hunt and the chase two (AD-19)
- **Errors:** a move outside its side's `moves` raises `InvalidMoveError`; a missing move for a living id, or a move for one that isn't, raises `WrongAgentsError`; either way the game doesn't change; `step` after the game ends raises `GameOverError`
- **Boundary:** snake, corridor, tank, hunt, line, chase, agent, pg, baselines, evaluate, train, replay, crossplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Env/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a recorded 6-step corridor game; a replay double of a recorded Snake game on seed 7 ending in a wall hit; a replay double of a recorded 2-point line game where one point is removed first; a replay double of a recorded 2-prey hunt game on seed 7 where the predator catches one prey
- **Used by:** evaluate, train, replay, registry, baselines, agent, pg, crossplay

### core.Step: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Step: obs: dict[int, np.ndarray]; rewards: dict[int, float]; alive: frozenset[int]; done: bool; scores: Scores`
- **Invariants:** `obs` and `rewards` hold one entry for each id that was sent a move; `alive` ⊆ those ids, the ids asked for moves next (CORE-08); `done` exactly when `alive` is empty, including at a time limit, and the end of a game empties it (the hunt's predator is alive until then); `scores` is every side's score so far
- **Boundary:** snake, corridor, tank, hunt, line, chase, evaluate, train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Step/ · **Cases:** a Snake step that eats food (+10); a game-ending Snake step (−10, done); a corridor step that reaches the right end (+1, done); a tank step where 1 of 3 fish is removed; a line step where the first point reaches 5; a hunt step where the predator catches 2 prey (+2) and a prey eats a pellet
- **Used by:** evaluate, train

### core.Circle: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Circle: x: float; y: float; r: float; color: str; fill: bool`
- **Invariants:** in the frame's cell units, origin at the top-left, y down; `color` is `#rrggbb`
- **Boundary:** tank, hunt, line, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Circle/ · **Cases:** the tank wall (radius 10 at (10, 10), not filled); the ball; a pellet
- **Used by:** core.Frame, replay

### core.Segment: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Segment: x1: float; y1: float; x2: float; y2: float; color: str`
- **Invariants:** as `Circle`'s units and colour
- **Boundary:** tank, hunt, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Segment/ · **Cases:** a fish's heading line; a prey's energy bar
- **Used by:** core.Frame, replay

### core.Frame: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Frame: cells: np.ndarray; colors: dict[int, str]; step: int; scores: Scores; shapes: tuple[Circle | Segment, ...] = (); caption: str = ""`
- **Invariants:** `cells` is H×W uint8 cell codes and sets the drawing area; every code has a `#rrggbb` colour in `colors`; `shapes` are drawn over the cells in order; `caption` is extra text shown with the step and scores (CORE-04, AD-06); Snake uses codes 0 empty, 1 body, 2 head, 3 food and no shapes; the tank uses a blank 20 × 20 grid, its wall, ball and each living fish with a heading segment, and the caption `fish left: N`; the hunt the same grid and wall, its 10 pellets, the predator with a heading segment and each living prey with a heading segment and an energy bar of length 0.6 × energy, and the caption `prey left: N`
- **Boundary:** snake, corridor, tank, hunt, line, chase, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Frame/ · **Cases:** the first frame of Snake seed 7; a 1 × 5 corridor frame; the first frame of tank seed 2 with 5 fish; the first frame of hunt seed 2 with 5 prey
- **Used by:** replay, evaluate

### core.Transition: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Transition: obs: np.ndarray; action: Move; reward: float; next_obs: np.ndarray; done: bool; agent: int = 0`
- **Invariants:** one id's step: `obs` and `next_obs` have the same length; `done` is true on the step that id is removed or the game ends; `agent` is the id it acted as
- **Boundary:** train, agent, pg · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Transition/ · **Cases:** a non-final Snake step with reward 0; a final Snake step with reward −10; a tank fish's removal step (reward 0, done, agent 2); a predator's catch step (reward +1, agent 0)
- **Used by:** agent, pg, train

### core.Agent: type
```python
class Agent(Protocol):
    name: str; kind: Literal["choices", "controls", "any"]
    def reset(self, seed: int) -> None: ...
    def act(self, obs: np.ndarray, env: Env, *, explore: bool) -> Move: ...
```
- **Invariants:** `kind` says which `Moves` it plays; `reset` is called once at the start of every game with that game's seed; `act` is called once per living id of the side it plays, in id order, on that id's observation, so one object plays every fish of a side with one policy (PG-04); with `explore=False`, the same seed and observations give the same moves (AGENT-03, PG-02, REPLAY-04)
- **Boundary:** agent, pg, baselines, evaluate, train, replay, selfplay, crossplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Agent/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a recorded random-agent game on corridor seed 3; a replay double of a recorded random-agent tank game on seed 2 with 2 fish
- **Used by:** evaluate, train, replay, registry, selfplay

### core.Lineup: type
`Lineup: TypeAlias = Mapping[str, Agent]`
- **Invariants:** one agent per side name of the environment it plays; one agent may be named for more than one side (AD-19)
- **Boundary:** evaluate, train, registry, selfplay, replay, crossplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Lineup/ · **Cases:** `{"snake": the scripted agent}`; `{"predator": a rung-3 agent, "prey": the scripted prey}`
- **Used by:** core.Match, core.check_lineup, evaluate, train, registry

### core.LearningAgent: type
```python
class LearningAgent(Agent, Protocol):
    def begin_game(self, game: int, n_games: int) -> None: ...
    def learn(self, t: Transition) -> float | None: ...
    def end_game(self) -> float | None: ...
    def save(self, path: Path) -> None: ...
```
- **Invariants:** called only for games in which its side learns: `begin_game` sets up training game `game` of `n_games`, `learn` is called for each of its side's ids' steps and `end_game` once after the game; each returns the loss of the update it made, or `None` when it made none (AD-12, AD-20); in a game where its side is frozen it only acts
- **Boundary:** agent, pg, train, selfplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.LearningAgent/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a recorded 3-game corridor DQN run, with losses from `learn`; a replay double of a recorded 3-game line run of rung 1, with losses from `end_game`
- **Used by:** train, registry, selfplay

### core.Match: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Match: agents: Lineup; learning: frozenset[str]; label: str = ""`
- **Invariants:** `agents` names one agent per side; each side in `learning` plays the run's own learner for that side, and only those learners are told about the game; every other side plays frozen (CORE-10, AD-20); `label` names the kind of game (`both`, `past-prey`, `past-predator`, `block-3`)
- **Boundary:** train, selfplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Match/ · **Cases:** a one-sided Snake game learning `{"snake"}`; the current predator against prey generation 3, learning `{"predator"}`; a taking-turns block-1 game learning `{"prey"}`
- **Used by:** core.Schedule, train, selfplay

### core.Schedule: type
```python
class Schedule(Protocol):
    def match(self, game: int) -> Match: ...
    def add_generation(self, side: str, number: int, path: Path) -> None: ...
```
- **Invariants:** `match` is called once before each training game, in game order; `add_generation` after each generation is saved, with its file; the same run seed gives the same matches (QR-05, AD-22)
- **Boundary:** train, selfplay, registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Schedule/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a recorded 20-game self-play schedule with generations added after games 10 and 20; a replay double of a recorded 20-game taking-turns schedule
- **Used by:** train, registry

### core.InvalidMoveError: type
`class InvalidMoveError(ValueError): agent: int; side: str; move: Move; allowed: Moves; step: int | None`
- **Invariants:** the message names the id, its side, the move and the allowed choices or each control with its limits, and, when raised by `evaluate.play_game`, the step number
- **Boundary:** snake, corridor, tank, hunt, line, chase, evaluate, train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.InvalidMoveError/ · **Cases:** Snake given move 3; the tank given a turn of 20; the predator given a turn of 12
- **Used by:** evaluate, train

### core.WrongAgentsError: type
`class WrongAgentsError(ValueError): missing: tuple[int, ...]; extra: tuple[int, ...]`
- **Invariants:** `missing` names the living ids with no move, `extra` the ids sent a move that aren't living; the message names each fish (TANK and HUNT edge cases)
- **Boundary:** tank, hunt, line, evaluate · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.WrongAgentsError/ · **Cases:** a move for removed fish 1; living fish 3 with no move; no move for the predator
- **Used by:** evaluate, train

### core.GameOverError: type
`class GameOverError(ValueError): ...`
- **Invariants:** the message says the game is over
- **Boundary:** snake, corridor, tank, hunt, line, chase, evaluate · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.GameOverError/ · **Cases:** a step after a Snake wall hit; a step after the tank's last fish is removed; a step after the hunt's last prey is removed
- **Used by:** evaluate

### core.EnvCheckError: type
`class EnvCheckError(TypeError): missing: tuple[str, ...]`
- **Invariants:** `missing` lists every absent behaviour by attribute name, and `sides` when the sides are empty or two share a name or an id
- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.EnvCheckError/ · **Cases:** an object missing `render` and `step`; two sides sharing id 0
- **Used by:** train, evaluate, replay

### core.MoveKindError: type
`class MoveKindError(TypeError): agent: str; agent_kind: str; side: str; env: str; env_kind: str`
- **Invariants:** the message names the agent and its kind, and the environment, the side and its kind (CORE edge case)
- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.MoveKindError/ · **Cases:** the DQN given the continuous tank; a rung given Snake
- **Used by:** train, evaluate, registry

### core.MissingSideError: type
`class MissingSideError(ValueError): env: str; sides: tuple[str, ...]`
- **Invariants:** `sides` names every side with no agent; the message names them and the environment (CORE edge case)
- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.MissingSideError/ · **Cases:** the hunt given only a predator; a lone agent given to the chase
- **Used by:** train, evaluate, registry

### core.NonFiniteLossError: type
`class NonFiniteLossError(FloatingPointError): update: int`
- **Invariants:** `update` is the agent's update count; `train.train_run` adds the game and step to the failed record's `error`
- **Boundary:** agent, pg, train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.NonFiniteLossError/ · **Cases:** a NaN loss on DQN update 12; an infinite loss on a rung's update 3
- **Used by:** agent, pg, train

### core.check_env: Start-up environment check
`def check_env(env: object) -> Env`
- **Invariants:** returns `env` unchanged when it has `name`, `sides`, `reset`, `step` and `render`, and its sides are non-empty with distinct names and disjoint ids
- **Errors:** `EnvCheckError` naming every missing behaviour, or `sides` (CORE edge case)
- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.check_env/ · **Cases:** a corridor env that passes; an object missing `render`; a two-sided env whose sides share id 0
- **Used by:** train, evaluate, replay; PL-01

### core.check_lineup: Start-up agents and environment match
`def check_lineup(agents: Agent | Lineup, env: Env) -> Lineup`
- **Invariants:** a lone `Agent` on a one-sided environment becomes `{side.name: agent}`; returns a lineup with one agent per side, each of kind `"any"` or matching its side's moves (`Choices` → `"choices"`, `Controls` → `"controls"`)
- **Errors:** `MissingSideError` naming each side with no agent, a lone agent on a two-sided environment included (CORE edge case); `MoveKindError` naming both kinds; `ValueError` naming a lineup key that isn't a side
- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.check_lineup/ · **Cases:** the random agent on the tank; the DQN on the continuous tank; the hunt with only a predator; both sides of the hunt at rung 3
- **Used by:** train, evaluate, registry, crossplay; PL-01

## snake

### snake.SnakeEnv: Classic Snake
`class SnakeEnv(Env): def __init__(self, size: int = 20) -> None; state: SnakeState; name = "snake"; sides = (Side("snake", 11, Choices(3), (0,)),)`
- **Invariants:** one id, 0; moves are 0 straight, 1 turn left, 2 turn right; observation order: danger straight, left, right; heading up, down, left, right; food up, down, left, right (SNAKE-06); rewards +10, −10 and 0 (SNAKE-07); the game ends after 100 × length steps without food (SNAKE-05); filling the grid ends the game as a win
- **Errors:** as `core.Env`
- **Boundary:** registry, baselines · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/snake.SnakeEnv/ · **Cases:** a replay double of seed 7 with fixed moves to a wall hit; a replay double of a game that eats 2 food
- **Used by:** registry, baselines; PL-01

### snake.SnakeState: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class SnakeState: body: tuple[tuple[int, int], ...]; heading: Literal["up", "down", "left", "right"]; food: tuple[int, int] | None; steps_since_food: int; won: bool`
- **Invariants:** `body[0]` is the head; cells are `(x, y)` with `0 ≤ x, y < size`; `food` is never on the body, and is `None` only after a win
- **Boundary:** baselines · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/snake.SnakeState/ · **Cases:** the start state of seed 7; a state with the head next to the right wall, heading right
- **Used by:** baselines

## arena

### arena.FishState: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class FishState: x: float; y: float; heading: float; speed: float`
- **Invariants:** tank units, origin at the centre, y up; `heading` in degrees anticlockwise from east; `speed` the last step's distance
- **Boundary:** tank, hunt · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/arena.FishState/ · **Cases:** a fish at the centre heading east at speed 0.3
- **Used by:** tank.TankState, hunt.HuntState

### arena.swim: Turn, then swim to at most the wall
`def swim(pos: np.ndarray, heading: np.ndarray, turn: np.ndarray, speed: np.ndarray, *, limit: float) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]`
- **Invariants:** for N fish (`pos` N × 2, the others N, angles in degrees), turns each by `turn` first, then moves it `speed` along the new heading, stopping where its centre reaches radius `limit` (the tank's radius less its own) with that heading; returns the new positions and headings and leaves the inputs unchanged (TANK-02, TANK-08, HUNT-02, AD-21)
- **Errors:** `ValueError` if the lengths disagree
- **Used by:** tank, hunt

### arena.ray_wall: Wall readings
`def ray_wall(pos: np.ndarray, heading: np.ndarray, *, radius: float = 10.0, reach: float = 4.0, rays: int = 8) -> np.ndarray`
- **Invariants:** N × `rays` readings; ray k points at heading + 360°·k / `rays`; each is the distance along it to the wall circle / `reach`, capped at 1 (TANK-07, HUNT-08, HUNT-09)
- **Used by:** tank, hunt

### arena.ray_circles: Nearest-circle readings
`def ray_circles(pos: np.ndarray, heading: np.ndarray, centres: np.ndarray, radii: np.ndarray, *, skip: np.ndarray | None = None, reach: float = 4.0, rays: int = 8) -> np.ndarray`
- **Invariants:** N × `rays` readings, rays as `ray_wall`'s: the distance along each ray to the nearest circle's edge / `reach`, 1 when none is within reach and 0 when the ray starts inside one; `skip[i]` is the index in `centres` of fish i itself, or −1, so a fish never sees itself (TANK-07, HUNT-08, HUNT-09); M = 0 circles gives all 1
- **Errors:** `ValueError` if the shapes disagree
- **Used by:** tank, hunt

### arena.random_spots: Random start spots
`def random_spots(rng: np.random.Generator, n: int, *, max_r: float, away: np.ndarray | None = None, min_dist: float = 0.0) -> np.ndarray`
- **Invariants:** n × 2 points drawn uniformly over the disc of radius `max_r`, each redrawn until it is at least `min_dist` from every point of `away`; draws only from `rng` (TANK-03, HUNT-01, HUNT-06)
- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `n` < 0, or if 10,000 draws find no spot for one point
- **Used by:** tank, hunt

## tank

### tank.TankEnv: Fish tank
```python
class TankEnv(Env):
    def __init__(self, fish: int = 5, *, compass: bool = False) -> None: ...
    state: TankState; name = "tank"
    def reset_to(self, state: TankState) -> dict[int, np.ndarray]: ...
```
- **Invariants:** one side, `Side("fish", 25, …, ids 0..fish-1)`; its moves are `Controls(("turn", "speed"), (-15.0, 0.0), (15.0, 0.3))`, turn in degrees, positive anticlockwise, applied before swimming (TANK-02), or with `compass` `Choices(8)`, 0 north then clockwise every 45°, at speed 0.3 (TWIN-01); movement, wall stop, rays and start spots by `arena` (AD-14); geometry, start, removal, wall stop and time limit follow TANK-01, TANK-03–TANK-06 and TANK-08, a hit on the last step removed first; rewards +1 per step survived and 0 on the removal step, score the mean steps survived (TANK-09)
- **Invariants (observation):** ray k points at heading + 45°·k (0 ahead, 1 front-left, …, 7 front-right); entries `3k`, `3k+1`, `3k+2` are its wall, ball-edge and nearest-other-fish distances over the 4-unit reach (1 = nothing within reach); entry 24 is speed / 0.3; the ball's motion isn't observed (TANK-07)
- **Invariants (`reset_to`):** starts a game from a hand-placed `state`, as if play had reached it, and returns each living fish's observation; the next `step` continues from `state.step` under the same rules, and the game's generator isn't drawn from (AD-18)
- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `fish` is outside 1–10; `reset_to` raises `ValueError` naming the fish or field if a fish id is outside `0..fish-1`, a fish lies outside radius 9.7, `survived` lacks a fish or `step` is outside 0–999; otherwise as `core.Env`, the message naming the control and its limits or the fish
- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/tank.TankEnv/ · **Cases:** a replay double of seed 2 with 5 fish and fixed moves until 2 fish are removed on one step; a 1-fish game on seed 7 to the 1,000-step limit; a compass game on seed 2
- **Used by:** registry; PL-01

### tank.TankState: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class TankState: step: int; ball: tuple[float, float]; ball_velocity: tuple[float, float]; fish: dict[int, FishState]; survived: dict[int, int]` with `FishState` as `arena.FishState`
- **Invariants:** positions in tank units, origin at the centre, y up, every fish within radius 9.7; `fish` holds the living fish only; `survived` holds every fish's steps survived so far
- **Boundary:** tank tests · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/tank.TankState/ · **Cases:** the start state of seed 2 with 5 fish; a ball about to bounce at 30° to the wall
- **Used by:** `tank.TankEnv.reset_to`, tank tests

## hunt

### hunt.HuntEnv: Predator-prey tank
```python
class HuntEnv(Env):
    def __init__(self, prey: int = 5) -> None: ...
    state: HuntState; name = "hunt"
    def reset_to(self, state: HuntState) -> dict[int, np.ndarray]: ...
```
- **Invariants:** sides `Side("predator", 17, Controls(("turn", "speed"), (-10.0, 0.0), (10.0, 0.35)), (0,))` and `Side("prey", 34, the tank's controls, ids 1..prey)` (CORE-03); start per HUNT-01 by `arena.random_spots`; each step in order: every living fish turns then swims by `arena.swim` (predator limit 9.5, prey 9.7; HUNT-02, HUNT-03), the predator catches every prey closer than 0.8 (HUNT-04), each remaining prey within 0.5 of a pellet eats it, the closer prey winning and the lower id on a tie, and the pellet respawns at least 0.5 from every living prey (HUNT-06), then every prey loses energy and one at 0 starves (HUNT-05); ends with no prey left or after 1,000 steps, a removal on the last step counted first (HUNT-07); rewards and scores per HUNT-10 and HUNT-11; energy is an integer in thousandths (AD-21)
- **Invariants (observation):** ray k as the tank's; a prey's entries `4k`…`4k+3` are its wall, predator-edge, nearest-other-prey-edge and nearest-pellet-edge distances, 32 is speed / 0.3 and 33 energy (HUNT-08); the predator's `2k` and `2k+1` are its wall and nearest-prey-edge distances, and 16 is speed / 0.35 (HUNT-09)
- **Invariants (`reset_to`):** as `tank.TankEnv.reset_to`, from a `HuntState` (AD-18)
- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `prey` is outside 1–10; `reset_to` raises `ValueError` naming the fish or field if an id is outside the sides, a fish lies outside its limit, an energy is outside 1–1,000, there aren't 10 pellets or `step` is outside 0–999; otherwise as `core.Env`, the message naming the side, the control and its limits, or the fish
- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/hunt.HuntEnv/ · **Cases:** a replay double of seed 2 with 5 prey and fixed moves until the predator catches one; a replay double of a 1-prey game on seed 7 where the prey never eats and starves at step 500; two prey reaching one pellet on one step
- **Used by:** registry; PL-01

### hunt.HuntState: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class HuntState: step: int; predator: FishState; prey: dict[int, FishState]; energy: dict[int, int]; pellets: tuple[tuple[float, float], ...]; survived: dict[int, int]; caught: int` with `FishState` as `arena.FishState`
- **Invariants:** positions as `tank.TankState`'s, the predator within radius 9.5 and every prey within 9.7; `prey` and `energy` hold the living prey only, energy in thousandths 1–1,000; exactly 10 pellets; `survived` holds every prey's steps survived so far; `caught` is the predator's score
- **Boundary:** hunt tests · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/hunt.HuntState/ · **Cases:** the start state of seed 2 with 5 prey; a prey with energy 500 that ends its move 0.4 from a pellet; a prey 0.7 from the predator after a step
- **Used by:** `hunt.HuntEnv.reset_to`, hunt tests

## corridor

### corridor.CorridorEnv: 1 × 5 test environment
`class CorridorEnv(Env): def __init__(self, length: int = 5, max_steps: int = 20) -> None; name = "corridor"`
- **Invariants:** one side, `Side("walker", length, Choices(2), (0,))`, starting in cell 0; moves are 0 left, 1 right; reaching the last cell gives +1 and ends the game, and every other step gives 0; `max_steps` also ends the game; the observation is the one-hot position
- **Errors:** as `core.Env`
- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/corridor.CorridorEnv/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a 4-step walk right to the end
- **Used by:** registry, tests of CORE-05 and CORE-06; PL-01

## line

### line.LineEnv: Continuous, several-agent test environment
`class LineEnv(Env): def __init__(self, points: int = 2, length: float = 5.0, max_steps: int = 50) -> None; name = "line"`
- **Invariants:** one side, `Side("point", 1, Controls(("push",), (-1.0,), (1.0,)), ids 0..points-1)`; each point starts at 0 and observes its own position / `length`; each step a point moves by its push, held at 0 from below; a point reaching `length` gets +1 and is removed (CORE-08), every other step gives 0; the game ends when every point is removed or after `max_steps`; score is the number of points that reached the end (CORE-07)
- **Errors:** as `core.Env`
- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/line.LineEnv/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a game where point 0 reaches 5 first and point 1 two steps later
- **Used by:** registry, tests of CORE-07 and CORE-08; PL-01

## chase

### chase.ChaseEnv: Two-sided test environment
`class ChaseEnv(Env): def __init__(self, length: float = 5.0, max_steps: int = 20) -> None; name = "chase"`
- **Invariants:** sides `Side("chaser", 2, Controls(("push",), (-1.0,), (1.0,)), (0,))` and `Side("runner", 2, the same controls, (1,))`; each observes its own position / `length`, then the other's; the chaser starts at 0, the runner at `length`; each step both move by their push, held within `[0, length]`; when they are within 0.5 after a step the chaser gets +1, the runner 0 and the game ends; otherwise the runner gets +1 and the chaser 0; `max_steps` also ends it; scores are the chaser's touches (0 or 1) and the runner's steps untouched (CORE-10)
- **Errors:** as `core.Env`
- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/chase.ChaseEnv/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a game where the chaser touches the runner on step 4; a replay double of a game where the runner lasts all 20 steps
- **Used by:** registry, tests of CORE-10; PL-01

## agent

### agent.AgentConfig: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class AgentConfig: memory: bool = True; frozen_copy: bool = True; eps_decay: bool = True; hidden: int = 256; lr: float = 1e-3; batch: int = 64; memory_size: int = 100_000; gamma: float = 0.9; refresh_every: int = 1_000; eps_start: float = 1.0; eps_end: float = 0.01; eps_fixed: float = 0.1`
- **Invariants:** the first 3 fields are the ablation switches (ABL-01–ABL-03); `eps_fixed` applies only when `eps_decay` is off; `0 < gamma < 1`
- **Boundary:** runs, train, study, registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/agent.AgentConfig/ · **Cases:** the default (full agent); each switch off alone; all 3 off
- **Used by:** runs, train, study, registry

### agent.DQNAgent: Hand-written DQN
```python
class DQNAgent(LearningAgent):
    def __init__(self, obs_size: int, n_actions: int, config: AgentConfig, *, seed: int) -> None: ...
    def values(self, obs: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray: ...
    @classmethod
    def load(cls, path: Path, side: Side) -> "DQNAgent": ...
```
- **Invariants:** `kind = "choices"`; `values` returns `n_actions` floats (AGENT-01); with `explore=False` it picks the highest value, ties going to the lowest index (AGENT-03); ε falls linearly from `eps_start` to `eps_end` over games `[0, n_games / 2)`, then stays at `eps_end` (AGENT-02); each `learn` remembers that id's step and makes one update, targets `r + gamma · max Q_frozen(s′)` or `r` when done, the frozen copy refreshed every `refresh_every` updates (AGENT-04, AGENT-05, TWIN-02); `end_game` returns `None`; memory off learns from the latest transition only (ABL-01), frozen copy off uses the current network (ABL-02); a loaded agent picks the same moves (AGENT-06)
- **Errors:** `load` raises `ValueError` naming both observation sizes and move counts if `side` doesn't match the file; `learn` raises `core.NonFiniteLossError` on a NaN or infinite loss or value
- **Boundary:** train, registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/agent.DQNAgent/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a recorded 3-game corridor training run; a saved full agent loaded back
- **Used by:** registry; PL-01, PL-04, PL-05

## pg

### pg.PGConfig: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class PGConfig: rung: Literal[1, 2, 3] = 3; value: bool = True; cap: bool = True; rescale: bool = True; hidden: int = 64; lr: float = 3e-4; gamma: float = 0.99; lam: float = 0.95; passes: int = 10; batch: int = 64; clip: float = 0.2; log_std_init: float = -0.5`
- **Invariants:** rung 1 is plain policy gradient, 2 actor-critic, 3 the limited-update agent (AD-13); `value`, `cap` and `rescale` are the tank's switches (ABL-06–ABL-08) and only rung 3 has them
- **Errors:** `ValueError` naming the switch and the rung if a switch is off on rung 1 or 2 (ABL edge case)
- **Boundary:** runs, study, registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/pg.PGConfig/ · **Cases:** each rung's default; rung 3 with each switch off alone; rung 1 with `cap` off (refused)
- **Used by:** runs, study, registry

### pg.Spread: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Spread: centre: np.ndarray; width: np.ndarray`
- **Invariants:** one entry per control; `width` > 0 is the standard deviation of a normal draw around `centre` (PG-01)
- **Boundary:** pg tests · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/pg.Spread/ · **Cases:** an untrained rung's spread on a tank observation
- **Used by:** pg tests

### pg.PGAgent: Hand-written policy-gradient ladder
```python
class PGAgent(LearningAgent):
    def __init__(self, obs_size: int, controls: Controls, config: PGConfig, *, seed: int) -> None: ...
    def policy(self, obs: np.ndarray) -> Spread: ...
    def value(self, obs: np.ndarray) -> float: ...
    @classmethod
    def load(cls, path: Path, side: Side, *, seed: int | None = None) -> "PGAgent": ...
```
- **Invariants:** `kind = "controls"`; one agent per side, learning only from the transitions it is handed, its own side's (PG-04, SELF-01); training moves are drawn from `policy(obs)`, evaluation moves are its centre, and both are clipped to the limits before they are sent (PG-01–PG-03); `learn` stores the step and returns `None`; `end_game` learns once from every stored step of every fish, then clears them (PG-04, PG-05), as AD-13 sets per rung and switch (PG-06–PG-09, ABL-06–ABL-08); a loaded agent picks the same moves (PG-12, SELF-07), and its training draws come from a generator built from `seed`, or from the saved run seed when `seed` is `None` (AD-22)
- **Errors:** `value` raises `ValueError` on a rung with no value estimate; `load` raises `ValueError` naming both observation sizes and controls if `side` doesn't match the file, another side's generation included (SELF edge case); `end_game` raises `core.NonFiniteLossError`
- **Boundary:** train, registry, selfplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/pg.PGAgent/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a recorded 3-game line run of each rung; a saved rung 3 loaded back; a saved predator generation loaded back; a prey generation loaded for the predator side (refused)
- **Used by:** registry; PL-01, PL-04, PL-05

### pg.draw: A training draw
`def draw(spread: Spread, rng: np.random.Generator) -> np.ndarray`
- **Invariants:** one float64 entry per control, `centre + width · z` with `z` standard normal from `rng`; not clipped (PG-01); the same `rng` state gives the same draw (AD-18)
- **Used by:** `pg.PGAgent`; pg tests (TC-22)

### pg.clip_move: A move clipped to the limits
`def clip_move(move: np.ndarray, controls: Controls) -> np.ndarray`
- **Invariants:** element-wise `min(max(move, low), high)`; a move already within the limits is returned unchanged (PG-03)
- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `move`'s length isn't `len(controls.names)`
- **Used by:** `pg.PGAgent`; pg tests (TC-22)

### pg.discounted_returns: One fish's returns
`def discounted_returns(rewards: Sequence[float], *, gamma: float = 0.99) -> np.ndarray`
- **Invariants:** one float64 entry per step, `G_t = r_t + gamma · G_{t+1}`, with nothing carried past the fish's last step, whether it was removed or reached the time limit (PG-05, PG-06, AD-13)
- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `rewards` is empty
- **Used by:** `pg.step_weights`; pg tests (TC-24)

### pg.advantages: One fish's blended advantages
`def advantages(rewards: Sequence[float], values: Sequence[float], *, gamma: float = 0.99, lam: float = 0.95) -> np.ndarray`
- **Invariants:** one float64 entry per step, the `lam`-blended sum of one-step errors `r_t + gamma · V_{t+1} − V_t`, with `V` after the fish's last step taken as 0 (PG-07, AD-13)
- **Errors:** `ValueError` if the two lengths differ or are 0
- **Used by:** `pg.step_weights`; pg tests (TC-24)

### pg.rescale: Advantages rescaled over one game
`def rescale(weights: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray`
- **Invariants:** `(weights − mean) / (std + 1e-8)`, population standard deviation over every entry given; the input isn't changed (PG-09)
- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `weights` is empty
- **Used by:** `pg.step_weights`; pg tests (TC-24)

### pg.step_weights: The weight of every step in one game
`def step_weights(rewards: Mapping[int, Sequence[float]], values: Mapping[int, Sequence[float]] | None, config: PGConfig) -> dict[int, np.ndarray]`
- **Invariants:** per fish, `discounted_returns` on rung 1 and on rung 3 with `value` off, `advantages` otherwise; on rungs 2 and 3 with `rescale` on, every fish's entries are rescaled together as one game (PG-06, PG-07, PG-09, ABL-06, ABL-08)
- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `values` is `None` where the config needs an estimate, or its fish or lengths differ from `rewards`
- **Used by:** `pg.PGAgent`; pg tests (TC-24)

### pg.capped_objective: Each move's objective
`def capped_objective(ratio: torch.Tensor, advantage: torch.Tensor, *, clip: float | None = 0.2) -> torch.Tensor`
- **Invariants:** element-wise `min(ratio · advantage, clamp(ratio, 1 − clip, 1 + clip) · advantage)`, or `ratio · advantage` with `clip=None` (cap off, ABL-07); not averaged; differentiable in `ratio` (PG-08, AD-13)
- **Errors:** `ValueError` if the shapes differ or `clip` isn't in (0, 1)
- **Used by:** `pg.PGAgent`; pg tests (TC-25)

## baselines

### baselines.RandomAgent: Uniform random baseline
`class RandomAgent(Agent): name = "random"; kind = "any"`
- **Invariants:** picks uniformly from `range(n)` for `Choices`, and each control uniformly within its limits for `Controls` (BASE-01), reading the limits of the side of the id it is asked for, with a generator reseeded by `reset(seed)`, so a replay on the same seed is identical (REPLAY-04)
- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.RandomAgent/ · **Cases:** a replay double of 10 moves on Snake seed 42; a replay double of 10 moves in the tank on seed 2; a replay double of 10 predator moves on hunt seed 2
- **Used by:** registry; PL-02, PL-05

### baselines.ScriptedAgent: Greedy, danger-avoiding Snake baseline
`class ScriptedAgent(Agent): name = "scripted"; kind = "choices"`
- **Invariants:** reads `env.state`; steps toward the food along the axis with the larger gap, and if that move is immediately fatal takes a non-fatal move, preferring straight; if every move is fatal it goes straight (BASE-02); deterministic
- **Errors:** `TypeError` naming the environment if `env` isn't a `SnakeEnv`
- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.ScriptedAgent/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a Snake seed 42 game; food ahead with a wall ahead
- **Used by:** registry; PL-02, PL-05

### baselines.ScriptedFish: Ball-fleeing tank baseline
`class ScriptedFish(Agent): name = "scripted"; kind = "controls"`
- **Invariants:** reads only the observation, in `tank.TankEnv`'s layout; always speed 0.3; with the wall within 1 unit ahead (forward wall reading ≤ 0.25), turns 15° toward the side whose rays read more room, left winning a tie; else away from the ray seeing the ball closest: rays 1–3 → −15°, rays 5–7 → +15°, ray 0 → toward the side with more room, ray 4 → straight; on equal ball readings the further-left ray counts; no ball in reach → straight (BASE-04); deterministic
- **Errors:** `TypeError` naming the environment if it isn't the continuous tank
- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.ScriptedFish/ · **Cases:** the ball on the front-left ray only; no ball and the wall 0.5 ahead with more room on the left; the ball and the wall ahead at once
- **Used by:** registry; PL-02, PL-05

### baselines.ScriptedPredator: Prey-chasing hunt baseline
`class ScriptedPredator(Agent): name = "scripted"; kind = "controls"`
- **Invariants:** reads only the observation, in `hunt.HuntEnv`'s predator layout; always speed 0.35; with a prey in reach, turns toward the ray seeing it closest: rays 1–3 → +10°, rays 5–7 → −10°, ray 0 → 0°, ray 4 → +10°; else with the wall within 1 unit ahead (forward wall reading ≤ 0.25), turns 10° toward the side whose rays read more room, left winning a tie; else straight (BASE-05); on equal prey readings the further-left ray counts; deterministic
- **Errors:** `TypeError` naming the environment and side if it isn't the hunt's predator
- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.ScriptedPredator/ · **Cases:** a prey on the front-left ray only; no prey and the wall 0.5 ahead with more room on the right; equal prey readings on rays 1 and 7
- **Used by:** registry; PL-02, PL-05

### baselines.ScriptedPrey: Wall-, predator- and pellet-led hunt baseline
`class ScriptedPrey(Agent): name = "scripted"; kind = "controls"`
- **Invariants:** reads only the observation, in `hunt.HuntEnv`'s prey layout; always speed 0.3; first, with the wall within 1 unit ahead, turns 15° toward the side with more room, left winning a tie; else with the predator in reach, away from the ray seeing it closest, as `ScriptedFish` flees the ball; else with a pellet in reach, toward the ray seeing it closest: rays 1–3 → +15°, rays 5–7 → −15°, ray 0 → 0°, ray 4 → +15°; else straight (BASE-06); on equal readings the further-left ray counts; deterministic
- **Errors:** `TypeError` naming the environment and side if it isn't the hunt's prey
- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.ScriptedPrey/ · **Cases:** the predator on the front-left ray only; no predator and a pellet on the right ray only; the predator and the wall ahead at once
- **Used by:** registry; PL-02, PL-05

## evaluate

### evaluate.EVAL_SEEDS: Held-out seeds
`EVAL_SEEDS: tuple[int, ...] = tuple(range(1_000_000, 1_000_100))`
- **Invariants:** 100 seeds; disjoint from every training seed (EVAL-01); regular evaluation points use the first `RunConfig.eval_games` of them (EVAL-02), and the cross-play table the first 20 (CROSS-01)
- **Boundary:** train, study, replay, crossplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.EVAL_SEEDS/ · **Cases:** the 100-seed tuple
- **Used by:** train, study, replay, crossplay

### evaluate.Seeds: type
`Seeds: TypeAlias = tuple[int, ...]`
- **Invariants:** game seeds in the order the games are played; every seed is in `[0, 1_000_000)`, so none is a held-out seed (EVAL-01)
- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.Seeds/ · **Cases:** the 1,000 training seeds of run seed 1; the 200 training seeds of run seed 2
- **Used by:** train

### evaluate.training_seeds: Training game seeds
`def training_seeds(run_seed: int, n_games: int) -> Seeds`
- **Invariants:** `n_games` seeds in `[0, 1_000_000)`, the same for the same `run_seed`
- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `n_games` < 1
- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.training_seeds/ · **Cases:** seed 1 with 1,000 games
- **Used by:** train; PL-01

### evaluate.GameResult: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class GameResult: seed: int; scores: Scores; length: int; frames: tuple[Frame, ...]`
- **Invariants:** `scores` holds every side's final score; `length` is the number of environment steps; `frames` is empty unless requested, otherwise `length + 1` frames from the first to the last
- **Boundary:** train, replay, crossplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.GameResult/ · **Cases:** a corridor game with frames; a Snake game without frames; a tank game with frames; a hunt game where the predator catches 3 and 2 prey starve
- **Used by:** train, replay, crossplay

### evaluate.EvalResult: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class EvalResult: seeds: tuple[int, ...]; scores: tuple[float, ...]; mean: float; std: float`
- **Invariants:** one side's scores, one per seed, in seed order; `std` is the population standard deviation
- **Boundary:** runs, train, study · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.EvalResult/ · **Cases:** the random agent on 100 held-out Snake seeds; a 10-seed corridor evaluation; the scripted fish on the first 20 held-out tank seeds; the scripted predator's catches against the scripted prey on 100 held-out hunt seeds
- **Used by:** runs, train, study

### evaluate.play_game: Play one game
`def play_game(env: Env, agents: Agent | Lineup, seed: int, *, explore: bool = False, frames: bool = False) -> GameResult`
- **Invariants:** `check_lineup` first; calls `env.reset(seed)` and each distinct agent's `reset(seed)` once, then each step asks every living id, in id order, its side's agent for a move and stops asking a removed one (CORE-08), until done; the same inputs give the same result
- **Errors:** `MissingSideError` and `MoveKindError` before the game; `InvalidMoveError` naming the id, the side, the move and the step number if a move is outside its side's moves (CORE edge case)
- **Boundary:** train, replay, crossplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.play_game/ · **Cases:** the scripted agent on Snake seed 42 with frames; an agent returning move 5; the random agent on line seed 3; the scripted predator against the scripted prey on hunt seed 2
- **Used by:** train, replay, crossplay, evaluate.evaluate; PL-01, PL-05

### evaluate.evaluate: Greedy score on a seed set
`def evaluate(env: Env, agents: Agent | Lineup, seeds: Sequence[int] = EVAL_SEEDS, *, side: str | None = None) -> EvalResult`
- **Invariants:** one `play_game(..., explore=False)` per seed, scoring `side`, or the only side when `None`; changes no agent state (EVAL-02, BASE-03)
- **Errors:** `MissingSideError` and `MoveKindError` before any game; `ValueError` if `side` is `None` on a two-sided environment or isn't one of its sides
- **Boundary:** train, study · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.evaluate/ · **Cases:** the random agent on the 100 held-out Snake seeds; a rung-3 predator against the scripted prey on the first 20 held-out hunt seeds, scoring `predator`
- **Used by:** train, study; PL-01, PL-02

## selfplay

### selfplay.SelfPlayConfig: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class SelfPlayConfig: rung: Literal["turns", "simultaneous", "selfplay"]; first: str; blocks: int = 10; both: float = 0.5`
- **Invariants:** `first` is the side that learns first when taking turns (the hunt: `prey`, SELF-03); `blocks` applies to `turns`; `both` is the share of self-play games played current against current, the rest split evenly between the two kinds against a past generation (SELF-05); `0 < both < 1`
- **Boundary:** runs, study, registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/selfplay.SelfPlayConfig/ · **Cases:** the hunt's `turns`, `simultaneous` and `selfplay` configs
- **Used by:** runs, study, registry

### selfplay.make_schedule: The match for each training game
`def make_schedule(config: SelfPlayConfig, learners: Mapping[str, LearningAgent], n_games: int, *, seed: int, load: Callable[[Path, str, int], Agent]) -> Schedule`
- **Invariants:** `turns`: game g is in block `g · blocks // n_games`, block 0 learning `first` and the sides alternating, the other side playing its current learner frozen (SELF-03); `simultaneous`: every game both current, both learning (SELF-04); `selfplay`: current against current while the opponent has no generation, else a kind drawn with shares `both`, `(1 − both) / 2`, `(1 − both) / 2`, and the past generation uniformly from those added so far, from a generator built from `seed`; a past generation is `load(path, side, seed)`ed once, cached, plays frozen and never learns (SELF-05, AD-22)
- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `learners` doesn't have exactly 2 sides, `first` isn't one of them, or `blocks` doesn't divide `n_games`
- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/selfplay.make_schedule/ · **Cases:** a 20-game self-play schedule on seed 1 with generations added after games 2 and 4; a 20-game taking-turns schedule; 10,000 self-play matches after 4 prey generations, for the counts SELF-05 checks
- **Used by:** registry; PL-01

## runs

### runs.AgentSpec: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class AgentSpec: kind: Literal["dqn", "pg", "random", "scripted"]; config: AgentConfig | PGConfig | None = None`
- **Invariants:** `config` is an `AgentConfig` exactly when `kind == "dqn"`, a `PGConfig` exactly when `"pg"`, else `None`; `dqn` and `pg` are learners (LOG-01)
- **Boundary:** train, study, registry, curves · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.AgentSpec/ · **Cases:** the full DQN; rung 3 with the cap off; the scripted baseline
- **Used by:** runs.RunConfig, study.Variant, registry

### runs.RunConfig: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class RunConfig: name: str; env: str; agents: dict[str, AgentSpec]; seed: int; n_games: int = 1_000; eval_every: int = 50; eval_games: int = 100; generations: int = 0; selfplay: SelfPlayConfig | None = None; env_options: tuple[tuple[str, int], ...] = ()`
- **Invariants:** `agents` has one entry per side of `env`; the learning sides are those with a learner; a baseline run has `n_games == 0` and no learner; `eval_games` ≤ 100; `generations` is 0 or divides `n_games`, and is 10 for every hunt training run (SELF-02); `selfplay` is set exactly when two sides learn (AD-20); `env_options` are `make_env`'s keyword arguments (tank: `fish`, `compass`; hunt: `prey`); `name` is a valid folder name (LOG-01)
- **Boundary:** train, study, registry, curves · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.RunConfig/ · **Cases:** the full Snake agent, seed 1; the memory-off agent, seed 3; the scripted Snake baseline; rung 3 with the cap off in a 5-fish tank, `eval_every` 100 and `eval_games` 20; a hunt self-play run on seed 2, both sides at rung 3 with 10 generations; the hunt's random-predator baseline (a random predator against the scripted prey)
- **Used by:** train, study, registry, curves

### runs.RunRecord: type
```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RunRecord:
    config: RunConfig; status: Literal["running", "finished", "failed"]; error: str | None; elapsed_s: float
    games: tuple[GameStat, ...]; evals: tuple[EvalPoint, ...]; losses: tuple[LossPoint, ...]
    final: dict[str, EvalResult] | None; generations: dict[str, tuple[int, ...]]
```
- **Invariants:** `games[i]` is training game i (LOG-02); an evaluation and a loss point every `eval_every` games (LOG-03); `final` holds one result per scored side over all `EVAL_SEEDS` (each learning side against the other sides' evaluation opponents; for a baseline, every side of its lineup) and is set exactly when `status == "finished"` (EVAL-02, AD-15); `generations` lists each learning side's saved generation numbers in order, empty when `config.generations` is 0 (SELF-02); `error` names the game and step when `status == "failed"`
- **Boundary:** curves, study, registry, replay, crossplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.RunRecord/ · **Cases:** a finished 200-game corridor run; a Snake run failed at game 430 (evaluation points to game 400); a baseline record; a record with no evaluation points; a finished tank run with 10 points over 20 seeds and a final over 100; a finished hunt self-play run with 10 points and 10 generations per side; a hunt run failed after 6 generations
- **Used by:** curves, study, registry, crossplay

### runs.GameStat: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class GameStat: scores: Scores; length: int`
- **Invariants:** every side's score; `length` ≥ 1
- **Boundary:** curves, study · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.GameStat/ · **Cases:** a Snake game scoring 4 in 212 steps; a tank game scoring 550 in 1,000 steps; a hunt game, predator 3 and prey 412, in 730 steps
- **Used by:** curves, study

### runs.EvalPoint: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class EvalPoint: game: int; results: dict[str, EvalResult]`
- **Invariants:** `game` is the number of training games played before this evaluation, a multiple of `eval_every`; one result per learning side
- **Boundary:** curves, study · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.EvalPoint/ · **Cases:** the Snake evaluation after game 50; a hunt evaluation after game 100 with both sides
- **Used by:** curves, study

### runs.LossPoint: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class LossPoint: game: int; mean_loss: dict[str, float]`
- **Invariants:** per learning side, the mean of the losses its learner's `learn` and `end_game` returned over the last `eval_every` games; NaN for a side that never learned in that window
- **Boundary:** curves, study · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.LossPoint/ · **Cases:** the Snake loss after game 100; a taking-turns window where the predator didn't learn
- **Used by:** curves, study

### runs.RunExistsError: type
`class RunExistsError(FileExistsError): name: str`
- **Invariants:** the message names the existing run and its environment
- **Boundary:** train, study, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.RunExistsError/ · **Cases:** creating `snake/full-s1` twice
- **Used by:** train, study, cli

### runs.RunWriter: type
```python
class RunWriter(Protocol):
    path: Path
    def add_game(self, scores: Scores, length: int) -> None: ...
    def add_eval(self, game: int, results: Mapping[str, EvalResult]) -> None: ...  # also flushes
    def add_loss(self, game: int, mean_loss: Mapping[str, float]) -> None: ...
    def save_agent(self, side: str, agent: LearningAgent) -> Path: ...
    def save_generation(self, side: str, number: int, agent: LearningAgent) -> Path: ...  # also flushes
    def finish(self, status: Literal["finished", "failed"], error: str | None = None, final: Mapping[str, EvalResult] | None = None) -> RunRecord: ...
```
- **Invariants:** `record.json` is atomically rewritten after every `add_eval` and `save_generation` and in `finish` (LOG-05); `save_agent` and `save_generation` write the files `agent_path` names (LOG-04, SELF-02); `finish("finished")` requires `final`; a failed run keeps the generations saved so far (SELF edge case)
- **Boundary:** train, study · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.RunWriter/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a recorded 100-game run with 2 evaluation points and a final; a replay double of a recorded 20-game chase run with 2 generations per side
- **Used by:** train, study; PL-01

### runs.agent_path: Where a saved agent lives
`def agent_path(run: Path, side: str, generation: int | None = None) -> Path`
- **Invariants:** `run/<side>.pt` for the final agent, `run/generations/<side>-<NN>.pt` for generation NN, two digits (LOG-04, AD-05)
- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `generation` is outside 1–99
- **Boundary:** registry, crossplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.agent_path/ · **Cases:** `runs/snake/full-s1`, `snake`; `runs/hunt/selfplay-s1`, `prey`, generation 3
- **Used by:** registry, crossplay

### runs.create_run: Start a run record
`def create_run(config: RunConfig, root: Path = Path("runs")) -> RunWriter`
- **Invariants:** creates `root/config.env/config.name/` and writes the config to the record at once (LOG-01, AD-15)
- **Errors:** `RunExistsError` naming the existing run if the folder exists; nothing is overwritten
- **Boundary:** train, study · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.create_run/ · **Cases:** a new corridor run; an existing run name
- **Used by:** train, study; PL-01, PL-02

### runs.load_record: Read a run record
`def load_record(path: Path) -> RunRecord`
- **Errors:** `FileNotFoundError` naming the run if `record.json` is missing
- **Boundary:** curves, study, registry, crossplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.load_record/ · **Cases:** a finished corridor record; a record failed at game 430; a finished hunt record
- **Used by:** curves, study, registry, crossplay; PL-02, PL-03, PL-04, PL-05

## train

### train.train_run: Train one run's learners
`def train_run(env: Env, learners: LearningAgent | Mapping[str, LearningAgent], config: RunConfig, *, opponents: Lineup = {}, schedule: Schedule | None = None, root: Path = Path("runs")) -> RunRecord`
- **Invariants:** checks `env` and the lineup first; plays `config.n_games` games on `training_seeds(config.seed, n_games)`, each game `schedule.match(game)`'s match, or every learner playing and learning when `schedule` is `None`; for learning sides `begin_game`, then each step `act(explore=True)` per living id in id order, `env.step`, `learn` per learning side's `Transition` in id order, and `end_game` after the game (AD-12, AD-20); every `eval_every` games records, per learning side, `evaluate` on the first `eval_games` held-out seeds against `opponents` for the other sides, and each side's mean loss; every `n_games / generations` games saves a generation of each learning side and passes it to `schedule.add_generation` (SELF-02); finishes with `final` over all `EVAL_SEEDS`, reusing the last point when it already is one, and saves each learner; same config → same record apart from `elapsed_s` (QR-05); works for any `Env` (CORE-05, CORE-07, CORE-10)
- **Errors:** `EnvCheckError`, `MoveKindError`, `MissingSideError` (a side with no learner, or, in a two-sided run, no evaluation opponent) and `RunExistsError` before any game; on any later failure, including `NonFiniteLossError` and `InvalidMoveError`, finishes the record as `failed`, naming the game and step, keeps its generations, then re-raises
- **Boundary:** study, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/train.train_run/ · **Cases:** a 200-game corridor run, seed 1; a run that hits a non-finite loss; a 100-game line run of rung 1; a 50-game chase run with both sides learning; a chase run whose schedule keeps the runner frozen
- **Used by:** study, cli; PL-01, PL-04

## registry

### registry.make_env: Environment by name
`def make_env(name: str, **options: int) -> Env`
- **Invariants:** `"snake"` → `SnakeEnv()`, `"corridor"` → `CorridorEnv()`, `"tank"` → `TankEnv(fish=options.get("fish", 5), compass=bool(options.get("compass", 0)))`, `"hunt"` → `HuntEnv(prey=options.get("prey", 5))`, `"line"` → `LineEnv()`, `"chase"` → `ChaseEnv()`; the result passes `check_env`
- **Errors:** `KeyError` naming the known environments; `TypeError` naming an option the environment doesn't take
- **Boundary:** study, replay, crossplay, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.make_env/ · **Cases:** `snake`; `corridor`; `tank` with 3 fish; `tank` with `compass=1`; `hunt` with 5 prey; `chase`; an unknown name
- **Used by:** study, replay, crossplay, cli; PL-01, PL-02, PL-04, PL-05

### registry.EvalSchedule: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class EvalSchedule: every: int; games: int`
- **Invariants:** `every` ≥ 1; 1 ≤ `games` ≤ 100; fills `RunConfig.eval_every` and `RunConfig.eval_games` (AD-15)
- **Boundary:** study, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.EvalSchedule/ · **Cases:** Snake's `(50, 100)`; the tank's `(100, 20)` for 1,000 games; the tank's `(1, 20)` for 5 games
- **Used by:** study, cli; PL-01, PL-04

### registry.eval_schedule: Evaluation cadence per environment
`def eval_schedule(env_name: str, n_games: int) -> EvalSchedule`
- **Invariants:** `EvalSchedule(50, 100)` for `snake` and `corridor`; `EvalSchedule(max(1, n_games // 10), 20)` for `tank`, `hunt`, `line` and `chase` (EVAL-02, AD-15)
- **Errors:** `KeyError` naming the known environments
- **Boundary:** study, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.eval_schedule/ · **Cases:** snake with 1,000 games; tank with 1,000 games; tank with 5 games; hunt with 1,000 games
- **Used by:** study, cli; PL-01, PL-04

### registry.make_agent: One side's learner for a run
`def make_agent(config: RunConfig, env: Env, side: str) -> LearningAgent`
- **Invariants:** from `config.agents[side]` and `env`'s side of that name: `"dqn"` → `DQNAgent(side.obs_size, side.moves.n, spec.config, seed=config.seed)`; `"pg"` → `PGAgent(side.obs_size, side.moves, spec.config, seed=config.seed)`, so the predator and the prey each get their own (SELF-01); the result passes `check_lineup` for that side
- **Errors:** `MoveKindError` naming both kinds (the DQN on the continuous tank, a rung on Snake); `ValueError` for a baseline kind or a name that isn't a side
- **Boundary:** study, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.make_agent/ · **Cases:** the full DQN on Snake; rung 3 on the tank; the DQN on the compass tank; the DQN on the continuous tank (refused); rung 3 for the hunt's predator
- **Used by:** registry.make_run, study, cli; PL-01, PL-04

### registry.RunSetup: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class RunSetup: env: Env; learners: dict[str, LearningAgent]; opponents: Lineup; schedule: Schedule | None`
- **Invariants:** `learners` has one learner per learning side of the config; `opponents` holds, for a two-sided environment, each side's scripted agent, or the random agent where the side has none (the chase), and is empty for a one-sided one; `schedule` is set exactly when `config.selfplay` is (AD-20)
- **Boundary:** study, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.RunSetup/ · **Cases:** the full DQN on Snake; a hunt self-play run with both learners, both scripted opponents and its schedule; a chase run
- **Used by:** study, cli; PL-01, PL-04

### registry.make_run: Everything a training run needs
`def make_run(config: RunConfig) -> RunSetup`
- **Invariants:** `make_env(config.env, **dict(config.env_options))`, `make_agent` for each learning side, the opponents, and with `config.selfplay` `selfplay.make_schedule(config.selfplay, learners, config.n_games, seed=config.seed, load=…)`, the loader loading a generation with `load_agent`'s rules (AD-22)
- **Errors:** as `make_env` and `make_agent`
- **Boundary:** study, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.make_run/ · **Cases:** the full Snake agent; a hunt self-play run on seed 2; a hunt simultaneous run
- **Used by:** study, cli; PL-01, PL-04

### registry.load_agent: Agent by name, run folder or generation
`def load_agent(spec: str, env: Env, side: str | None = None) -> Agent`
- **Invariants:** for `side`, or the only side when `None`: `"random"` gives `RandomAgent`; `"scripted"` gives `ScriptedAgent` on Snake, `ScriptedFish` on the continuous tank, and `ScriptedPredator` or `ScriptedPrey` on the hunt's sides (AD-08); a run folder gives that side's final agent and `<run folder>@<n>` its generation n, loaded with `DQNAgent.load` or `PGAgent.load` as the record's `AgentSpec` says (SELF-07, REPLAY-01)
- **Errors:** `FileNotFoundError` naming the run or generation if it doesn't exist or has no saved agent; `ValueError` naming the environment and side if `"scripted"` has none there, or if `side` is `None` on a two-sided environment
- **Boundary:** replay, study, crossplay, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.load_agent/ · **Cases:** `random`; `scripted` on snake; `scripted` on the tank; `scripted` for the hunt's prey; a saved DQN run folder; a saved rung 3 run folder; `runs/hunt/selfplay-s1@10` for the predator; a missing run
- **Used by:** replay, study, crossplay, cli; PL-02, PL-05

## curves

### curves.CurvePlot: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class CurvePlot: path: Path; lines: tuple[str, ...]; skipped: tuple[str, ...]`
- **Invariants:** `lines` are the drawn labels, baselines included; `skipped` names every record with no evaluation points
- **Boundary:** study, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/curves.CurvePlot/ · **Cases:** the full agent with 3 ablations and 2 baselines; one record skipped; the 3 rungs with 2 baselines; the predator's 3 self-play rungs with 2 baselines
- **Used by:** study, cli

### curves.plot_curves: Learning-curve chart
`def plot_curves(records: Sequence[RunRecord], out: Path, *, baselines: Sequence[RunRecord] = (), side: str | None = None) -> CurvePlot`
- **Invariants:** draws `side`'s evaluation results, or the only side's when `None`; one line per config minus seed: the mean evaluation score at each evaluation point, with a min–max band across seeds (CURVE-01, CURVE-02); each baseline record whose other sides all play `scripted` as a labelled flat line of `side`'s final mean (CURVE-03); labels name the agent and each switch that is off: `full` or the DQN's switches off (CURVE-04), a rung's name (`reinforce`, `actor-critic`, `ppo`) plus any switch off (CURVE-06), a self-play rung's name (`turns`, `simultaneous`, `selfplay`, CURVE-07), `compass` added for the compass tank; reads only records, in ≤ 10 s (CURVE-05)
- **Errors:** `ValueError` naming the environments if the records come from more than one, or naming the sides if `side` is `None` for a two-sided environment or isn't one of its sides
- **Boundary:** study, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/curves.plot_curves/ · **Cases:** the full agent, 3 ablations and 2 baselines; 5 seeds of each rung and 2 tank baselines; 5 seeds of each self-play rung for the prey with the 3 hunt baseline records; records from two environments
- **Used by:** study, cli; PL-03, PL-04

## replay

### replay.Replay: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Replay: agents: dict[str, str]; env: str; seed: int; frames: tuple[Frame, ...]; scores: Scores`
- **Invariants:** `agents` is the spec each side was loaded from; `frames` runs from the first frame to game over; `scores == frames[-1].scores`
- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.Replay/ · **Cases:** the scripted agent on Snake seed 42; the random agent on corridor seed 3; the scripted fish on tank seed 2; predator generation 10 against prey generation 3 on hunt seed 42
- **Used by:** cli

### replay.record_replay: Re-play a game
`def record_replay(agents: str | Mapping[str, str], env_name: str, seed: int, **options: int) -> Replay`
- **Invariants:** a lone spec on a one-sided environment, or one per side (REPLAY-01); `make_env(env_name, **options)`, taking the first run folder's own `env_options` when none are given, then `load_agent` per side and `play_game(..., explore=False, frames=True)`; the same inputs give identical frames (REPLAY-04); on a held-out seed a side's score equals its logged evaluation score against the same opponent (REPLAY-05)
- **Errors:** as `registry.load_agent` and `registry.make_env`, and `MissingSideError` for a side with no spec, raised before any window opens
- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.record_replay/ · **Cases:** the scripted agent on Snake seed 42; a saved rung on tank seed 1,000,000; predator generation 10 against prey generation 3 on hunt seed 42
- **Used by:** cli; PL-05

### replay.Playback: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Playback: frames: tuple[int, ...]; fps: tuple[int, ...]`
- **Invariants:** one entry per frame shown, in order: `frames[i]` indexes `Replay.frames` and `fps[i]` is the speed when it was shown, within 1–30; the two have the same length; a pause adds no entry, and a step while paused adds exactly the next index (REPLAY-03)
- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.Playback/ · **Cases:** a 3-frame replay played through at 10 fps; paused after frame 0 then stepped once; sped up to 30 fps after frame 1
- **Used by:** cli

### replay.show: Replay window
`def show(replay: Replay, *, fps: int = 10) -> Playback`
- **Invariants:** draws each frame's cells, then its shapes, with the step, each side's score and the caption (REPLAY-02); space pauses, → steps exactly one frame while paused, ↑/↓ change the speed within 1–30 fps (REPLAY-03); stays on the last frame until closed, then returns what it showed; reads keys only from the pygame event queue, so a test drives it headless with SDL's dummy video driver and posted key events
- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `fps` is outside 1–30
- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.show/ · **Cases:** a 3-frame corridor replay shown headless; a 3-frame tank replay shown headless; a 3-frame hunt replay shown headless
- **Used by:** cli; PL-05

## crossplay

### crossplay.CrossTable: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class CrossTable: run: str; rows: str; cols: str; row_gens: tuple[int, ...]; col_gens: tuple[int, ...]; seeds: tuple[int, ...]; scores: dict[str, np.ndarray]; complete: bool`
- **Invariants:** `rows` is the predator side and `cols` the prey side (CROSS-02); `seeds` are the first 20 `EVAL_SEEDS`; `scores[side]` is float64 shaped `(len(row_gens), len(col_gens), len(seeds))`, that side's score in each game; `complete` exactly when both sides have 10 generations (CROSS edge case)
- **Boundary:** study, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/crossplay.CrossTable/ · **Cases:** a full 10 × 10 table; a table from a run with 6 generations per side (incomplete)
- **Used by:** study, cli; PL-04

### crossplay.build_table: Every generation against every other
`def build_table(run: Path, *, games: int = 20) -> CrossTable`
- **Invariants:** reads the run's record, loads every saved generation with `registry.load_agent(f"{run}@{n}", env, side)`, plays each row generation against each column generation on the first `games` held-out seeds with `play_game(..., explore=False)` (CROSS-01), and writes `run/crossplay.json`; never trains (CROSS-02, AD-23)
- **Errors:** `FileNotFoundError` naming the run; `ValueError` naming the run if its environment hasn't two sides or a side has no generation
- **Boundary:** study, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/crossplay.build_table/ · **Cases:** a finished 20-game chase run with 2 generations per side; a run with no generations (refused)
- **Used by:** study, cli; PL-04

### crossplay.load_table: Read a saved table
`def load_table(path: Path) -> CrossTable`
- **Errors:** `FileNotFoundError` naming the run if `crossplay.json` is missing
- **Boundary:** study, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/crossplay.load_table/ · **Cases:** a saved 10 × 10 table
- **Used by:** study, cli; PL-04

### crossplay.write_table: One rung's table for the notes
`def write_table(tables: Sequence[CrossTable], out: Path, *, title: str) -> Path`
- **Invariants:** writes one Markdown table to `out`: a row per predator generation, a column per prey generation, each cell `predator mean / prey mean` over every table's games (CROSS-02); a generation missing from a table is averaged over the tables that have it, and the incomplete runs are listed under it
- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `tables` is empty or mixes environments
- **Boundary:** study, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/crossplay.write_table/ · **Cases:** 5 full tables of one rung; 4 full tables and one incomplete
- **Used by:** study, cli; PL-04

## study

### study.Variant: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Variant: agents: dict[str, AgentSpec]; selfplay: SelfPlayConfig | None = None; generations: int = 0`
- **Invariants:** one `AgentSpec` per side of the plan's environment; fills `RunConfig`'s fields of the same names
- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/study.Variant/ · **Cases:** Snake's `no-memory`; the tank's `ppo`; the hunt's `selfplay`
- **Used by:** study.StudyPlan

### study.StudyPlan: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class StudyPlan: env: str; full: str; learners: tuple[str, ...]; ablations: tuple[str, ...]; configs: dict[str, Variant]; env_options: tuple[tuple[str, int], ...]; sides: tuple[str, ...]; ladder: bool; optional: tuple[str, ...]; crossplay: bool; sections: tuple[str, ...]`
- **Invariants:** `configs` has one entry per variant name; `full` is in `learners`, the agents compared with both baselines, in rung order; `ladder` compares each learner with the one before; `ablations` are compared with `full`; `optional` learners may be skipped (SELF-06); `crossplay` builds each run's table and compares each side's last generation with its first; `sections` are the notes headings required (AD-16)
- **Invariants (plans):** `STUDIES["snake"]`: `full` / (`full`,) / (`no-memory`, `no-frozen`, `no-decay`), sides (`snake`,), no ladder; `STUDIES["tank"]`: `ppo` / (`reinforce`, `actor-critic`, `ppo`) / (`no-value`, `no-cap`, `no-rescale`), 5 fish, sides (`fish`,), a ladder; `STUDIES["hunt"]`: `selfplay` / (`turns`, `simultaneous`, `selfplay`) / (), both sides rung 3 with 10 generations, 5 prey, sides (`predator`, `prey`), `turns` optional, cross-play, sections "Why it learns", "Why self-play works" and one per rung (AD-24)
- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/study.StudyPlan/ · **Cases:** `STUDIES["snake"]`; `STUDIES["tank"]`; `STUDIES["hunt"]`
- **Used by:** study.run_study, study.check_notes; PL-04, PL-06

### study.Comparison: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Comparison: label: str; verdict: Literal["beats", "does not beat", "better", "hurts learning", "later beats earlier", "no detectable difference", "missing"]; method: str; statistic: float | None; p_value: float | None; p_adjusted: float | None`
- **Invariants:** baseline comparisons use "beats", "does not beat" or "missing" (EVAL-03); ladder comparisons "better", "no detectable difference" or "missing" (PG-10); ablation comparisons "hurts learning", "no detectable difference" or "missing" (ABL-04); generation comparisons "later beats earlier" or "no detectable difference" (CROSS-03); "missing" has no statistics; in a two-sided environment `label` names the side
- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/study.Comparison/ · **Cases:** the agent beats the random baseline; an ablation with no detectable difference; a missing ablation; rung 3 better than rung 2; the self-play prey's later generation beats its earlier
- **Used by:** cli

### study.compare_scores: One-sided comparison (null-hypothesis-testing)
`def compare_scores(full: np.ndarray, other: np.ndarray, *, label: str, n_comparisons: int, alpha: float = 0.05) -> Comparison`
- **Invariants:** `full` and `other` are each k × 100 (finished training seed × held-out seed, 1 ≤ k ≤ 5; a baseline is 1 × 100), columns in `EVAL_SEEDS` order; pairs by held-out game on each side's per-game mean over its rows; tests whether `full` is higher, corrected for `n_comparisons`; `method` states the seeds each side used (AD-09); the test is built with the **`null-hypothesis-testing`** skill
- **Errors:** `ValueError` if the column counts differ, or if either side has no rows or more than 5
- **Used by:** study.run_study; PL-04

### study.compare_generations: Last generation against first (null-hypothesis-testing)
`def compare_generations(last: np.ndarray, first: np.ndarray, *, label: str, n_comparisons: int, alpha: float = 0.05) -> Comparison`
- **Invariants:** `last` and `first` are each k × 10 × 20 (complete run seed × opponent generation × held-out game, 1 ≤ k ≤ 5) of one side's scores, rows the same runs in the same order; tests whether `last` is higher, paired by run, opponent generation and game, corrected for `n_comparisons` (CROSS-03, AD-23); `method` states the runs used; the test is built with the **`null-hypothesis-testing`** skill
- **Errors:** `ValueError` if the shapes differ or k is outside 1–5
- **Used by:** study.run_study; PL-04

### study.StudyReport: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class StudyReport: env: str; baselines: tuple[Comparison, ...]; ladder: tuple[Comparison, ...]; ablations: tuple[Comparison, ...]; crossplay: tuple[Comparison, ...]; spreads: dict[str, float]; incomplete: tuple[str, ...]; report: Path; charts: tuple[Path, ...]; tables: tuple[Path, ...]`
- **Invariants:** 2 baseline comparisons per learner and side, one ladder comparison per rung above the first in a ladder, 3 ablation comparisons, 2 cross-play comparisons per learner (Snake: 2, 0, 3, 0; tank: 6, 2, 3, 0; hunt: 12, 0, 0, 6, or 8, 0, 0, 4 without `turns`); `spreads` is each learner's (and side's) standard deviation of its per-seed mean final score (PG-10); `incomplete` names runs left out (EVAL and CROSS edge cases); a missed bar the spec calls a finding is stated as one and the study still succeeds (ABL-05, PG-11, SELF-08); `charts` is `curves.png` plus `ladder.png` in a ladder, or `curves-<side>.png` per side (CURVE-07); `tables` is one `crossplay-<rung>.md` per rung with runs
- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/study.StudyReport/ · **Cases:** a complete Snake study; a study where all 3 ablations show no detectable difference; a study with a failed ablation run; a tank study where rung 3 shows no detectable difference; a hunt study without `turns` where the simultaneous predator shows no detectable difference
- **Used by:** cli

### study.run_study: The full study
`def run_study(env_name: str = "snake", seeds: Sequence[int] = (1, 2, 3, 4, 5), *, n_games: int = 1_000, workers: int | None = None, skip: Sequence[str] = (), root: Path = Path("runs")) -> StudyReport`
- **Invariants:** trains every variant of `STUDIES[env_name]` but the `skip`ped optional ones, for each seed in parallel processes, on `registry.eval_schedule`'s cadence; scores the baselines on `EVAL_SEEDS`; builds each finished run's cross-play table in the same pool when the plan has cross-play; compares on each run's `final` and tables, correcting within each family (AD-16); a skipped learner leaves every family and table (SELF-06); writes `root/<env>/study/report.md`, its charts and tables; runs unattended (QR-02)
- **Errors:** a failed run is reported as missing or incomplete, never as a difference: a learner's unfinished runs are left out of its baseline and cross-play comparisons, which use its finished ones; an ablation, or a rung in a ladder comparison, with any unfinished run is `missing` (AD-09); `ValueError` naming the learner if `skip` names one that isn't optional; `RuntimeError` naming the runs only if `full` has no finished run
- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/study.run_study/ · **Cases:** a 2-seed, 100-game corridor study with one ablation run failing; the same study with one full-agent run failing; a 2-seed, 20-game line study of the 3 rungs; a 2-seed, 20-game chase study of the 3 self-play rungs with `turns` skipped
- **Used by:** cli; PL-04

### study.NotesCheck: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class NotesCheck: page: Path; exists: bool; missing_sections: tuple[str, ...]; empty_sections: tuple[str, ...]; broken_links: tuple[str, ...]; ok: bool`
- **Invariants:** required sections are the plan's `sections`, so a page checked before any study still gets a section check: "Why it learns" (EXPL-01), one per ablation under its variant name (EXPL-02), one per ladder rung after the first under its rung name (EXPL-04), and the hunt's "Why self-play works" and one per rung (EXPL-05), an optional rung's only once it has runs; `broken_links` names each of `runs/<env>/study/report.md`, its charts and its tables that the page doesn't link or whose link doesn't resolve from the page's folder (EXPL-03); when `exists` is false every required section is missing; `ok` is true exactly when the page exists and all three tuples are empty
- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/study.NotesCheck/ · **Cases:** a complete Snake page; a missing page; a page with an empty "Why it learns" section and no chart link; a tank page missing its `ppo` section; a hunt page missing its "Why self-play works" section and a cross-play table link
- **Used by:** cli; PL-06

### study.check_notes: Check the learner's notes page
`def check_notes(env_name: str, *, notes_root: Path, runs_root: Path) -> NotesCheck`
- **Invariants:** reads `notes_root/<env>.md` and `runs_root/<env>/study/report.md` only; a section is a Markdown heading of any level, matched case-insensitively, and is empty when no non-blank text comes before the next heading of the same or a higher level; never writes, creates or changes a file, so the page is byte-identical afterwards (AD-10)
- **Errors:** `KeyError` naming the studied environments for an environment with no `StudyPlan`; none for a missing page or a missing report, which show up in the result
- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/study.check_notes/ · **Cases:** a complete Snake page beside a study folder; no page; a page whose chart link points to a missing file; a complete tank page; a complete hunt page
- **Used by:** cli; PL-06

`add_generation` after each generation is saved, with its file; the same run seed gives the same matches (QR-05, AD-22)\n- **Boundary:** train, selfplay, registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Schedule/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a recorded 20-game self-play schedule with generations added after games 10 and 20; a replay double of a recorded 20-game taking-turns schedule\n- **Used by:** train, registry\n\n### core.InvalidMoveError: type\n`class InvalidMoveError(ValueError): agent: int; side: str; move: Move;
